The resulting equation is therefore a field-aligned radial model whose validity is limited to angularly localized states. Within this setting, we apply the indirect mode (or search mode) of Tridiagonal representation approach in the Laguerre and Jacobi bases. In the Laguerre basis, the tridiagonal constraint generates linear-plus-inverse and linear-plus-quadratic radial potentials, and the coefficient recurrences are connected with Meixner--Pollaczek and Meixner polynomial type structures, therefore yielding the analytic spectra and expansion wavefunctions. In the Jacobi basis, the mapping of the radial half-line to a finite interval produces a singular confining effective potential and a three-term recurrence that is treated through a truncated generalized eigenvalue problem. The formulation provides analytically and numerically tractable benchmark models for field-modified potential functions, while keeping the assumptions, parameter restrictions, endpoint behavior, and convergence tests explicit. All energy spectra formulae obtained under the present models are new and are coupled with magnetic fields analytically and numerically when using the Laguerre bases and the Jacobi bases respectively.

</details>


### [105] [Eigenvalue-by-Eigenvalue Comparison of a Sierra--Rodríguez-Laguna-Type Spectrum with the Riemann Zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23353)
*Mi-Ra Hwang,Eylee Jung,MuSeong Kim,DaeKil Park*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究SR型Hamiltonian $\widehat H_{SR}=xp$的自伴延拓，其离散谱由$\mathrm{Re}[e^{-iθ/2}K_{1/2+iE/2}(2π)]=0$确定。对$θ=1.417π$数值求解得前606个本征值$E_n$，并逐一与Riemann zeta函数前606个非平凡零点的纵坐标$γ_n$比较；用最速下降法与Riemann–von Mangoldt公式导出$E_n-γ_n$的Lambert-$W$闭式预测，最终将数值偏差定位到计数函数$N(T)$在$T=γ_n$处的中点约定，修正后预测与Odlyzko前$10^5$个零点数据吻合至$0.03\%$以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sierra–Rodríguez-Laguna型$xp$ Hamiltonian的自伴延拓具有与Riemann zeta零点对应的离散谱，为研究Hilbert–Pólya猜想提供具体模型。此前数值比较中出现$E_n$与$γ_n$的偏差，需判断其来源：是Bessel函数渐近式的误差，还是计数函数约定处理不当。

Method: 对$\mathrm{Re}[e^{-iθ/2}K_{1/2+iE/2}(2π)]=0$在$θ=1.417π$下数值求解，取前606个$E_n$；采用最速下降（鞍点）法处理修正Bessel函数，并结合Riemann–von Mangoldt公式，导出$E_n-γ_n$的Lambert-$W$函数闭式表达；将所得$K_{1/2+iE/2}(2π)$领头相位与已知大虚阶Bessel函数严格渐近公式比对；最后重新审视计数函数$N(T)$在$T=γ_n$处的处理方式。

Result: $K_{1/2+iE/2}(2π)$的领头相位与已知严格渐近公式精确一致，排除Bessel函数为偏差来源；偏差源于波动项$S(T)$的中点约定使有效量子数偏移$1/2$。修正后$γ_n\sim g(n-11/8)$（而非$g(n-7/8)$），$E_n-γ_n$的新预测与Odlyzko前$10^5$个零点实测值的偏差在$0.03\%$以内。

Conclusion: SR型$xp$ Hamiltonian的谱与Riemann zeta零点对应中的数值不匹配，并非来自Bessel函数渐近误差，而是计数函数$N(T)$在零点处的中点约定问题；采用$γ_n\sim g(n-11/8)$后，$E_n-γ_n$的Lambert-$W$闭式预测与大规模零点数据高度吻合（$<0.03\%$），支持该自伴延拓模型作为研究zeta零点的有效框架。

Abstract: We study the self-adjoint extension of the Sierra--Rodríguez-Laguna (SR) $H=xp$-type Hamiltonian $\widehat H_{SR}$. Its discrete spectrum is fixed by the equation $\mathrm{Re}\!\left[e^{-iθ/2}K_{1/2+iE/2}(2π)\right]=0$. We solve this equation numerically for $θ=1.417π$ and obtain the first 606 eigenvalues $E_n$. We compare them, one by one, with the ordinates $γ_n$ of the first 606 nontrivial zeros of the Riemann zeta function. Using the steepest-descent (saddle-point) method for the modified Bessel function, together with the Riemann--von Mangoldt formula, we derive a closed-form prediction for $E_n-γ_n$ in terms of the Lambert-$W$ function. We show that our leading-order phase for $K_{1/2+iE/2}(2π)$ matches exactly a known, rigorous asymptotic formula for modified Bessel functions of large imaginary order. This rules out the Bessel function as the source of a numerical mismatch we found earlier. We then trace that mismatch to how the counting function $N(T)$ must be treated exactly at $T=γ_n$: the usual midpoint convention for the fluctuating term $S(T)$ shifts the effective quantum number by $1/2$. With this fix, $γ_n\sim g(n-11/8)$ instead of $g(n-7/8)$, and the new prediction for $E_n-γ_n$ matches, to within $0.03\%$, the value we measure directly from the first $10^5$ tabulated zeta zeros (A.~Odlyzko).

</details>


### [106] [Unified Spectral, Dynamical, and Correlation Signatures of an Exceptional Point in Cavity Optomechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23382)
*Khazali Fahmi,Ahmad R. T. Nugraha,Ferry A. A. Nugraha,Adam B. Cahaya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究耗散腔光机械系统中例外点（EP）结构如何传递到实验可观测的动力学、谱学与量子统计量，并给出可测量的EP指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米系统中的例外点是本征值与本征矢同时简并的奇点，但已有研究多停留在理论谱结构层面；作者希望回答EP是否以及如何被实验可及的动力学、光谱和量子关联观测量所"继承"，从而为腔光机械平台上的非厄米物理提供可验证判据。

Method: 在光-机械共振条件下，将系统约化为有效非厄米一阶矩（first-moment）动力学并分析其二阶EP；通过求解瞬态光子/声子布居、一阶相干、谱极点及二阶强度关联，考察平方根型劈裂、非振荡到阻尼振荡的转变以及Jordan块导致的多项式-指数弛豫；再利用量子回归定理与高斯矩因子化推导稳态涨落，检验连接一阶与二阶关联的Siegert关系。

Result: 在光学-机械共振处存在二阶EP，扰动下本征值劈裂呈特征平方根依赖；该平方根律同样支配瞬态光子与声子布居、一阶相干、谱极点及二阶强度关联。EP以下动力学为非振荡，EP以上出现阻尼振荡与谱极点频率劈裂；在EP处Jordan块给出多项式-指数弛豫与二阶谱极点。稳态涨落满足Siegert关系；零延迟自相关保持热值，而有限延迟强度关联呈现清晰的EP特征。

Conclusion: 该工作将非厄米模式简并（EP）与腔光机械中可测量的动力学及关联观测量直接联系起来，表明有限延迟二阶关联等量子统计量可作为EP的实验探针。

Abstract: We investigate how the exceptional-point structure of a dissipative cavity optomechanical system is inherited by experimentally accessible dynamical, spectral, and quantum-statistical observables. At optical-mechanical resonance, the effective non-Hermitian first-moment dynamics exhibits a second-order exceptional point, where two eigenvalues and their eigenvectors coalesce and the eigenvalue splitting follows the characteristic square-root dependence on perturbations. We show that the same square-root feature also governs transient photon and phonon populations, first-order coherence, spectral poles, and second-order intensity correlations. Below the exceptional point, the dynamics is non-oscillatory, whereas above it damped oscillations emerge together with frequency splitting of the spectral poles. At the exceptional point, the Jordan-block structure produces polynomial-exponential relaxation and a second-order spectral pole. Using the quantum regression theorem and Gaussian moment factorization, we further show that the stationary fluctuations satisfy the Siegert relation linking first- and second-order correlations. Although the zero-delay autocorrelations retain their thermal value, the finite-delay intensity correlations exhibit clear exceptional point signatures. This finding connects non-Hermitian mode coalescence with measurable dynamical and correlation observables in cavity optomechanics.

</details>


### [107] [Quantum scattering by intersecting δ-potential barriers: from Gaudin's kaleidoscope to quantum Galperin billiards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23406)
*Yi-Cong Yu,Wen-Jie Qiu,Xiaoming Cai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了平面波在Gaudin万花筒模型（共点相交的直线δ势垒）上散射的一般框架：投影Lippmann-Schwinger方程分解为奇异部分、由几何移动规则生成的有限极点闭合和正则余项，出射态为圆上的原子测度。在π/N特殊角处通道数固定，一般角处通道生灭相变但不留奇异概率迹，仅由相移携带，并可精确求解量子Galperin台球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究共点相交δ势垒的散射问题，试图超越坐标Bethe拟设的适用范围，揭示通道生灭与相移之间的量子-经典对应关系，并统一处理Gaudin万花筒与量子Galperin台球等模型。

Method: 构建投影Lippmann-Schwinger方程框架，将其分解为奇异部分、由几何移动规则生成的有限极点闭合（pole closure）和正则余项；出射态表示为圆上的原子测度；对量子Galperin台球利用镜像法化为单一线性系统，从而给出相移的闭式解。

Result: 在特殊角π/N处（相交势垒生成二面体群D_N），通道数保持固定；在一般角处通道被创造和湮灭，从零权重平滑开启。这些相变在概率中不留奇异迹，而由相移携带，体现超越坐标Bethe拟设的量子-经典对应。同一方程还可精确求解量子Galperin台球。

Conclusion: 该框架统一描述了共点相交δ势垒的散射，揭示了通道数与角度相关的几何规则及其由相移承载的效应，为Gaudin万花筒和量子Galperin台球提供了精确可解的处理途径。

Abstract: We develop a general framework for the scattering of a plane wave by a class of Gaudin kaleidoscope models: straight $δ$-potential barriers intersecting at a common point. The projected Lippmann-Schwinger equations split into a singular part, a finite pole closure generated by a geometric moving rule, and a regular remainder, the outgoing state being an atomic measure on the circle. At the special angles $π/N$, where the intersecting barriers generate the dihedral group $D_N$, the number of channels stays fixed, whereas at generic angles channels are created and destroyed, opening smoothly from zero weight as a critical direction is crossed. This leaves no singular trace in the probabilities, being carried instead by the phase shifts, a quantum-classical correspondence beyond the reach of the coordinate Bethe ansatz. The same equations solve the quantum Galperin billiards exactly, the method of images reducing them to a single linear system whose phase shifts follow in closed form.

</details>


### [108] [Feedback-Induced Dynamical Phases in a Self-Adaptive Quantum Kicked Rotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23420)
*Pan Gao,Zheng-Wei Zhou,Guang-Can Guo,Xi-Wang Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于量子受踢转子的自适应Floquet系统，踢力强度由腔介导反馈自洽生成；超辐射转变产生对称/反对称两条不稳定通道，统一组织非平衡Floquet相，并揭示共振弹道输运、反共振、周期倍增局域化及随反馈增强的交叉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子受踢转子中踢力强度为外部静态参数，缺乏自洽动力学反馈，难以描述驱动与内部自由度耦合产生的非平衡相。作者希望建立自适应Floquet框架，用腔介导反馈使踢力成为动力学变量，从而统一理解输运、局域化与时间序。

Method: 构建腔介导自洽反馈的量子受踢转子模型：踢力强度作为动力学变量由反馈生成；超辐射转变诱导腔介导踢力，形成对称与反对称两条竞争不稳定通道；分别在共振踢力与非公度踢力条件下分析其竞争、周期倍增、反共振及涨落效应。

Result: 共振踢力下，两通道竞争产生双踢动力学，支持共振弹道输运，并出现周期四倍转子演化的反共振。非公度踢力下，反对称不稳定性稳定出鲁棒周期倍增局域相，保持持续次谐波动力学。反馈增强时，关联时间涨落逐渐抑制量子干涉，驱动从周期倍增局域化到不规则局域化、再到次扩散输运的交叉。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了自适应量子混沌动力学的一般框架，表明动力学反馈可从根本上重塑驱动量子系统中的输运、局域化与时间序，并为非平衡Floquet相提供统一组织原理。

Abstract: We introduce a self-adaptive Floquet system based on a quantum kicked rotor, in which the kicking strength itself becomes a dynamical variable generated self-consistently through cavity-mediated feedback. A superradiant transition gives rise to cavity-mediated kicking and two competing instability channels, symmetric and antisymmetric, which provide a unified organizing principle for the nonequilibrium Floquet phases. For resonant kicking, their competition produces double-kick dynamics that support resonant ballistic transport and an emergent antiresonance with period-quadrupled rotor evolution, arising from a balance between the two instability channels. Remarkably, for incommensurate kicking, the antisymmetric instability stabilizes a robust period-doubled localized phase with persistent subharmonic dynamics despite the underlying incommensurate driving, revealing localized temporal order absent in conventional kicked rotors. As the feedback strength increases, correlated temporal fluctuations progressively suppress quantum interference, driving crossovers from period-doubled localization to irregular localization and eventually to subdiffusive transport. Our results establish a general framework for self-adaptive quantum-chaotic dynamics and demonstrate how dynamical feedback can fundamentally reshape transport, localization, and temporal order in driven quantum systems.

</details>


### [109] [Liouvillian Response for Temporal Information in Quantum Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23429)
*Jiande Cao,Rui-Yang Gong,Zhongjin Lin,Yexiong Zeng,Ze-Liang Xiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立了一个微观"响应—性能"框架，将Liouvillian动力学直接与量子储备池计算（QRC）的任务性能联系起来，揭示开放系统动力学如何决定任务相关信息流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统为量子储备池计算提供了时间信息处理的物理基础，但其动力学与计算性能之间的微观机制尚不清楚，缺乏将二者直接关联的统一理论。

Method: 将可观测的Volterra权重分解为 $W^{(q)}=BH^{(q)}$，区分内部输入历史路径与其读出可见性；提出动力学响应须同时满足"输入生成、读出可见、目标相关、且可分辨高于正则化尺度"四个条件才能对任务有贡献；分析Liouvillian模式对任务相关信息的保持与传播作用，以及对称性对读出可见性的决定作用。

Result: 揭示了不同协方差模式编码不同时间信息结构；系统参数与处理协议可重塑其贡献以提升QRC性能；热化过程使储备池从保留输入历史转向由最近输入主导，随后正则化抑制残余信息；弱测量可通过消除对称性强制的通道等价性激活任务相关协方差模式；延迟反馈为历史信息创建受控返回路径。

Conclusion: 该框架确立了开放系统动力学与计算性能之间的微观联系，为在量子储备池中工程化任务相关信息流提供了原理性指导。

Abstract: Open quantum systems offer a physical substrate for temporal information processing in quantum reservoir computing, yet the microscopic mechanisms linking their dynamics to computational performance remain unclear. Here we establish a microscopic response-to-performance framework that connects Liouvillian dynamics directly to task performance. We decompose observable Volterra weights as $W^{(q)}=BH^{(q)}$, separating internal input-history pathways from their readout visibility, and show that a dynamical response contributes to a task only if it is input-generated, readout-visible, target-correlated, and resolvable above the regularization scale. This framework reveals how Liouvillian modes govern the retention and propagation of task-relevant information, while symmetries determine which components remain visible to the readout. It further establishes how distinct covariance modes encode different temporal information structures and how system parameters and processing protocols can reshape their contributions to improve QRC performance. Thermalization drives the reservoir from processing that retains input history to a response dominated by the most recent input before regularization suppresses the residual information. We finally show that weak measurement can activate task-relevant covariance modes by lifting symmetry-imposed channel equivalence, whereas delayed feedback creates controlled return pathways for historical information. These results establish a microscopic connection between open-system dynamics and computational performance, providing principles for engineering task-relevant information flow in quantum reservoirs.

</details>


### [110] [Entanglement Cost of Optimal Distributed Quantum State Purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23441)
*Jiayi Zhao,Chengkai Zhu,Xin Wang,Ge Bai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 确定在退极化噪声下，空间分离双方在LOCC限制下实现任意二分纯态全局最优概率性双拷贝纯化所需预共享纠缠量：d≥2时一对最大纠缠量子比特即足够；反之任何达到该基准的有限维资源态（即使允许PPT松弛）至少需1 ebit纠缠生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络与模块化量子计算机中的噪声管理需要明确纠缠资源的下限与充分条件，但空间分离双方受限于LOCC时达到全局最优纯化所需的预共享纠缠量此前缺乏精确标定。

Method: 研究退极化噪声下任意二分纯态的概率性双拷贝纯化问题，比较LOCC约束与全局最优成功变换；构造使用单个预共享最大纠缠量子比特对的局域受控-SWAP协议，并对达到同一基准的有限维预共享资源态在正部分转置（PPT）松弛下分析其纠缠生成下界。

Result: 每个局域维度d≥2，一对共享最大纠缠量子比特足以精确复现全局最优成功变换；反之，任何有限维预共享资源态即使允许PPT松弛，要达到该基准其纠缠生成至少为1 ebit。对含1 ebit的纯资源态或含混合态的双量子比特资源态，仅当态恰有两个非零Schmidt权重且均为1/2（即局域酉等价于最大纠缠量子比特对）时才能达到该基准。

Conclusion: 单个ebit是LOCC下实现该纯化基准的充要纠缠门槛，为评估量子网络与模块化量子计算机中噪声管理的纠缠需求提供了基准。

Abstract: We determine the preshared entanglement required for spatially separated parties, restricted to local operations and classical communication, to attain globally optimal probabilistic two-copy purification of arbitrary bipartite pure states under depolarizing noise. In every local dimension $d\ge 2$, one shared maximally entangled qubit pair suffices: local controlled-SWAP operations exactly reproduce the globally optimal successful transformation. Conversely, any finite-dimensional preshared resource state that attains the same benchmark, even under a positive-partial-transpose relaxation, must have entanglement of formation at least one ebit. For pure resources with one ebit, or for two-qubit resources including mixed states, attaining the benchmark is possible only for states with exactly two nonzero Schmidt weights, both equal to $1/2$, namely those locally unitarily equivalent to a maximally entangled qubit pair. These findings provide a benchmark for evaluating the entanglement demands of noise management in quantum networks and modular quantum computers.

</details>


### [111] [Zero-Knowledge Proofs of Quantumness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23455)
*Duong Hieu Phan,Weiqiang Wen,Xingyu Yan,Jinwei Zheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并形式化"量子性零知识证明"概念，以解决当前量子性证明中量子证明者可能被恶意经典验证者利用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算机快速发展，量子性证明成为重要方向。但现有方案中，经典验证者可通过恶意交互策略从量子证明者处提取有用信息，从而求解该方案所依赖的困难问题实例。根本原因是缺乏形式化定义来阻止恶意验证者获取信息。

Method: 作者形式化量子性零知识证明，要求经典验证者从与量子证明者交互中获得的信息不超过用模拟经典证明者与同一验证者交互所能模拟的信息。作者发现经典的零知识证明足以将现有量子性证明方案编译为量子性零知识证明方案，并主张在验证者侧而非证明者侧要求零知识更通用，可将验证者行为从恶意约束为诚实好奇。具体地，通过在验证者侧要求可提取的非交互零知识论证，可将基于Shor因式分解的方案与[Brakerski et al., FOCS, 2018]中基于LWE的方案转化为量子性零知识证明。

Result: Shor因式分解方案和LWE方案均可通过验证者侧的可提取NIZK论证转换为量子性零知识证明。

Conclusion: 量子性零知识证明可视为量子性证明的增强安全概念，使双方在量子性证明和经典零知识证明中分别承担双重角色。

Abstract: With the rapid development of quantum computers, proofs of quantumness have recently become an interesting research direction. However, in current schemes for proofs of quantumness, quantum provers face the risk of being maliciously exploited by classical verifiers. Through malicious strategies in interaction with quantum provers, classical verifiers could solve some instances of hard problems that arise from the specific scheme in use. This is due to the lack of formalization that prevents malicious verifiers from extracting useful information in proofs of quantumness. To address this issue, we formalize zero-knowledge proofs of quantumness. Intuitively, the zero-knowledge property necessitates that the information gained by the classical verifier from interactions with the quantum prover should not surpass what can be simulated using a simulated classical prover interacting with the same verifier. As a result, the new zero-knowledge notion can prevent a malicious verifier from exploiting quantum advantage. We find that the classical zero-knowledge proof is sufficient to compile some existing proofs of quantumness schemes into zero-knowledge proofs of quantumness schemes. It appears to be more general to require zero-knowledge proof on the verifier side instead of the prover side. This helps to regulate the verifier's behavior from malicious to be honest-but-curious. As a result, both parties will play not only one role in the proofs of quantumness but also the dual role in the classical zero-knowledge proof. Specifically, Shor's factoring-based scheme and the learning with errors-based scheme in [Brakerski et al., FOCS, 2018] can be transformed into zero-knowledge proofs of quantumness by requiring an extractable non-interactive zero-knowledge argument on the verifier side. Zero-knowledge proofs of quantumness can thus be viewed as an enhanced security notion for proofs of quantumness.

</details>


### [112] [Quantum Teleportation Over a Noisy Relay Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23459)
*Yigal Ilin,Uzi Pereg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究含噪中继场景下的量子隐形传态与贝尔测量问题，提出两类封闭式容量公式的量子中继信道模型，并给出基于压缩转发的最优编码方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信协议（隐形传态与超密编码）在存在噪声的中继环境下性能受限，需要建立可解析刻画的中继信道模型并确定其容量，以指导实际量子中继网络的编码设计。

Method: 引入两种量子中继信道模型：发送端发送一个量子比特，中继获知信道中泡利误差的边信息，并通过正交的速率受限链路与接收端通信；提出压缩-转发编码方案，将泡利误差序列压缩为若干bin并传送bin索引，接收端利用该部分误差信息降低有效信道噪声后再进行量子译码。

Result: 为两类中继信道模型分别推导出封闭形式的容量公式，并通过匹配的逆定理（converse bounds）证明压缩-转发策略达到最优。

Conclusion: 所提出的压缩-转发策略在两模型中均达到容量最优，闭式容量结果刻画了中继边信息与受限经典链路对量子通信速率的精确影响。

Abstract: Quantum teleportation and Bell measurements are considered in a noisy relay setting. We introduce two quantum relay-channel models with closed-form capacity formulas, motivated by the canonical quantum communication protocols of teleportation and superdense coding. In both models, the sender transmits a qubit, while the relay observes side information about the Pauli errors that occur in the channel and communicates with the receiver through an orthogonal rate-limited link. We develop a compress-forward coding scheme in which the relay compresses the Pauli-error sequence into bins and sends the bin index to the receiver. The receiver uses this partial error information to reduce the effective channel noise before quantum decoding. We show that this strategy is optimal by establishing matching converse bounds for both models.

</details>


### [113] [Comparative Study of Quantum and Classical Machine Learning Models in Binary Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23476)
*Anand Kumar Mishra,Ramanuj Awasthi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在乳腺癌威斯康星数据集上对比了两种量子分类器（VQC、QSVM）与三种经典分类器（逻辑回归、SVM、MLP），结果显示经典方法在准确率与训练时间上仍占优，量子模型仅在良性类召回率上表现有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子机器学习（QML）能否借助量子计算提升计算效率与模型表达能力，并检验其在小规模结构化数据上是否真正超越调优良好的经典分类器。

Method: 在Breast Cancer Wisconsin数据集上，将量子分类器Variational Quantum Classifier（VQC）和Quantum Kernel SVM（QSVM）与三种经典基线（Logistic Regression、SVM、Multi-Layer Perceptron）进行对比；量子电路基于PennyLane框架构建，并在经典后端进行模拟，以准确率、召回率和训练时间作为评价指标。

Result: 经典Logistic Regression准确率最高，达97.8%；经典SVM与QSVM均为95.6%；VQC准确率较低为88.9%，但其良性类召回率达100%，恶性类召回率约70.6%（17例恶性仅正确识别12例）。训练时间上量子模型劣势明显：QSVM耗时23.29秒，而经典线性模型不足0.01秒。

Conclusion: 在小规模结构化数据集上，基于量子计算的分类器尚未超越调优良好的经典方法；其在良性类召回率等方面具备一定竞争力，但恶性类召回率（尤其VQC）弱于经典基线，未来值得在真实量子计算机上进一步研究。

Abstract: A potential path forward is Quantum Machine Learning (QML), which aims to leverage quantum computing in conjunction with classical machine learning to enhance computing efficiency and the expressiveness of models. In this paper, two different quantum classifiers - Variational Quantum Classifier (VQC) and Quantum Kernel Support Vector Machine (QSVM) - are compared with three classical classifiers as baseline classifiers - Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machine (SVM), and a Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) - on the Breast Cancer Wisconsin dataset. The quantum circuits were created in the PennyLane framework and simulated on a classical backend. However, in terms of accuracy, classical Logistic Regression performed better with an accuracy of 97.8%, classical SVM and QSVM with an accuracy of 95.6% each, although the Quantum VQC achieved a lower accuracy of 88.9% and had a recall of 100% for the benign class, though it correctly identified only 12 of the 17 malignant cases (a malignant-class recall of approximately 70.6%). The drawback of quantum models is the higher training time; however, since the quantum circuit needs to be classically simulated, the quantum SVM took 23.29 seconds compared to less than 0.01 seconds for the classical linear models. These results indicate that for small structured datasets, classifiers based on quantum computing have not yet surpassed well-tuned classical counterparts. In some respects (e.g., benign-class recall), they perform competitively, though not on malignant-class recall, where the VQC in particular performed worse than the classical baselines, which is worth further investigation on real quantum computers.

</details>


### [114] [Strong Symmetry from Two-Point Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23591)
*Han Yan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明两体关联（等价于所有两点约化密度矩阵）可精确确定混态的连续强对称性，并给出从两点关联提取最大连通强对称子李群及特征的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强/弱对称性及其自发破缺可区分混态中的对称性与有序形式，但如何从可实验获取的观测量精确判定强对称性尚不清晰，全态层析又难以实现。

Method: 利用完整的一点与两点关联函数（等价于所有两体约化密度矩阵），针对连通群的连续在格点幺正作用，证明相同关联的态具有相同强对称性及特征；并设计算法从两点关联确定最大连通强对称子李群及其特征。另分析群与表示特定的少关联充分条件。

Result: 证明两体关联可唯一决定连续强对称性与特征；构造出从两点关联提取最大连通强对称子李群及特征的算法；识别出若干群与表示相关、只需更少关联即可判定的情形。

Conclusion: 两点关联比全态层析更易获取，故该判定与算法可直接应用于凝聚态系统、量子模拟器与量子电路等实验平台。

Abstract: Strong and weak symmetries and their spontaneous breaking distinguish different forms of symmetry and order in mixed states. We show that complete one- and two-point correlation functions, equivalently all two-site reduced density matrices, determine exact strong symmetry for continuous onsite unitary actions of connected groups: states with identical correlations have the same strong symmetry and character. We also present an algorithm to determine the maximal connected strong-symmetry sub-Lie group and its character from two-point correlations. Because such correlations are more accessible than full-state tomography, the algorithm is readily applicable to experiments ranging from condensed-matter systems to quantum simulators and circuits. We also identify group and representation-specific cases in which fewer correlations suffice.

</details>


### [115] [Casimir force in a water nanolayer confined by graphene sheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23609)
*Wijnand Broer,Jure Dobnikar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了被纳米级液态水层隔开的两层石墨烯间的卡西米尔力，发现限域效应与界面效应对力的影响随水层厚度呈现非单调变化，石墨烯电子响应的纳米限域不产生影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡西米尔力强烈依赖于相互作用材料的电磁极化率，而现有研究多采用体材料模型处理中间介质，忽略了纳米受限水层的限域效应与界面效应，因此需要厘清纳米薄层水对石墨烯间卡西米尔力的具体影响。

Method: 理论计算被纳米级液态水薄层分隔的两层石墨烯之间的卡西米尔力，并将结果与体材料模型（bulk model）对比，分别考察水层厚度依赖的限域效应和界面效应，同时单独评估纳米限域对石墨烯电子响应的影响。

Result: 在水层厚度不超过3.4 nm时，限域效应使卡西米尔力相比体材料情形最多降低26%；在更大厚度下，体材料模型会低估卡西米尔力，最多达42%，这源于界面效应。该效应随厚度非单调变化：在70 nm处达到最大值，随后缓慢衰减，直到2微米才趋近体材料极限。纳米限域对石墨烯电子响应的改变对卡西米尔力没有影响。

Conclusion: 纳米受限液态水层显著且非单调地调制石墨烯间的卡西米尔力，体材料描述在从纳米到微米尺度范围内均不适用；而石墨烯电子响应的限域效应可被忽略。

Abstract: Casimir forces, arising from quantum and thermal fluctuations, depend strongly on the electromagnetic susceptibilities of the interacting materials. Here, we theoretically investigate the Casimir force between two graphene sheets separated by a nanometer-thin layer of liquid water. We find that, at thicknesses up to 3.4 nm, the confinement reduces the Casimir force by up to 26 % compared to the bulk case. At larger thicknesses, the bulk model underestimates the Casimir force by up to 42 % due to the interface effect. This effect does not decrease monotonically as a function of the layer thickness: it increases up to a maximum at 70 nm and it slowly declines to the bulk limit only at 2 microns. We separately assess the effect of nanoscale confinement on the electronic response of graphene, which has no effect on the Casimir force.

</details>


### [116] [Spectral width and polynomial degree in perfect state transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23633)
*Xingkun Song*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在多项式哈密顿量（有界度数与谱宽）下研究完美态转移的最短时间，给出最优时间的整数插值刻画、若干图类的度界与渐近结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化在受限哈密顿量（多项式形式、有界度数m与谱宽W）下实现完美态转移所需的最短时间，理解离散/图的量子态转移的时间下界与最优构造。

Method: 将最优转移时间刻画为π/W的整数倍：对强共谱对，用指定奇偶性的整数插值求解；对等间距且符号交替的特征值，分析相位多项式的度界与仿射极小元；用定量Chebyshev稳定性估计研究近极小相位多项式；并对超立方体（奇素数维）、J(2m,m)互补顶点等具体图类构造与分析多项式哈密顿量。

Result: 强共谱对的最优时间（有限时）为π/W的整数倍；对等间距交替符号特征值给出保证极小元为仿射的度界及每个固定精确度的尖锐渐近；近极小相位多项式满足定量Chebyshev稳定性；确定奇素数维超立方体每个度界下的最优转移时间；J(2m,m)互补顶点在固定谱宽下的最优时间在可行度区间内随m指数增长；并构造出所有可行度m-t（t=o(m)）均可实现亚指数转移时间的多项式哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 多项式哈密顿量下的最优态转移时间由离散整数插值与相位多项式逼近决定，呈现度界—谱宽—转移时间之间的权衡，并在超立方体与Johnson图等具体结构中给出尖锐或指数级结论。

Abstract: We study the minimum time for perfect state transfer under polynomial Hamiltonians with bounded degree and spectral width. For a strongly cospectral pair and width bound $W$, the optimum, when finite, is an integer multiple of $π/W$, determined by integer interpolation with prescribed parities. For equally spaced supported eigenvalues with alternating signs, we give degree bounds under which every minimizer is affine, and sharp asymptotics for each fixed exact degree. Near-minimizing phase polynomials satisfy a quantitative Chebyshev stability estimate. We determine the optimal transfer time for every degree bound on hypercubes of odd prime dimension. For complementary vertices of $J(2m,m)$, the optimal time at fixed spectral width grows exponentially in $m$ throughout an interval of feasible degrees. We also construct polynomial Hamiltonians showing that every feasible degree $m-t$ with $t=o(m)$ admits subexponential transfer time.

</details>


### [117] [Dynamics of nonclassicality in a generalized Tavis Cummings model with XY spin $(1/2,1)$ atomic interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23648)
*Bouchra El Alaoui,Abdallah Slaoui,Rachid Ahl Laamara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究广义JC与TC模型中非经典性的动力学，用Wigner-Yanase偏斜信息量化，揭示了共振条件下激发交换与非经典性增强，以及纵向磁场作为有效失谐导致场非经典性显著降低的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究腔量子电动力学中光与物质相互作用的非经典性动力学，特别是集体原子自由度及XY自旋相互作用对非经典性演化的影响，以明确自旋与场非经典性究竟由激发流与共振条件还是初始能量分布主导。

Method: 采用Wigner-Yanase偏斜信息作为非经典性度量。首先构建单个玻色腔模耦合自旋1原子的广义JC模型，分析腔场与原子子系统的非经典性时间演化；随后引入额外自旋1/2原子扩展为广义TC模型，考察集体原子自由度的影响。在含与不含XY自旋相互作用两种情形下研究全局动力学，并调节纵向磁场与共振条件。

Result: 共振条件下，腔场与原子子系统之间发生高效激发交换，产生规则、相干的非经典性动力学并增强场的非经典性生成。增大纵向磁场主要起有效失谐作用而非直接交换相互作用，使原子-场耦合显著减弱，激发局域于原子子系统，抑制向腔场的相干能量转移，导致场非经典性明显下降，而量子关联大部分保留在原子子系统中。原子叠加态表现出高度不规则的 spins 动力学，反映失谐引起的量子关联重分布。

Conclusion: 自旋与场非经典性动力学主要由相互作用介导的激发流和共振条件决定，而非仅由初始能量分布决定；纵向磁场通过有效失谐调控激发局域化与量子关联在原子与场之间的分配。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of nonclassicality, quantified by the Wigner Yanase skew information, in generalized Jaynes Cummings (JC) and Tavis Cummings (TC) models describing light matter interactions in cavity quantum electrodynamics. We first analyze a generalized JC model consisting of a single bosonic cavity mode coupled to a spin 1 atom, focusing on the temporal evolution of nonclassicality in both the cavity field and the atomic subsystem. We then extend this framework to a generalized TC model by introducing an additional spin $1/2$ atom, enabling us to examine the influence of collective atomic degrees of freedom. The global dynamics are investigated both with and without an $XY$ spin interaction. Our results show that, under resonance conditions, the system exhibits efficient excitation exchange between the cavity field and the atomic subsystem, resulting in regular, coherent nonclassicality dynamics and enhanced field nonclassicality generation. Conversely, an increasing longitudinal magnetic field acts primarily as an effective detuning parameter rather than a direct exchange interaction. In this off resonant regime, the atom field coupling is substantially weakened, localizing excitations within the atomic subsystem. This localization suppresses coherent energy transfer to the cavity field, leading to a pronounced reduction in field nonclassicality, while the atomic subsystem retains the majority of the quantum correlations. Notably, atomic superposition states exhibit highly irregular spin dynamics, reflecting a detuning-induced redistribution of quantum correlations. These findings demonstrate that the dynamics of spin and field nonclassicality are governed primarily by interaction mediated excitation flow and resonance conditions rather than the initial energy distribution alone, ..

</details>


### [118] [Nonequilibrium energy transport and fluctuations in two-photon-driven nonlinear quantum optical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23651)
*Y. T. Chen,Y. W. Lu,J. C. Lu,C. Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在旋转框架下结合全计数统计建立驱动量子主方程，统一描述驱动辅助的非相干跃迁，揭示非线性量子光学系统中双光子驱动导致的多光子共振能量流增强及涨落调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非经典光驱动的非线性量子光学系统中，非平衡输运与涨落的理解具有挑战性；现有理论往往忽略驱动引起的频率移动，难以统一刻画驱动辅助的非相干过程。

Method: 在旋转框架下构建驱动量子主方程，并与全计数统计相结合；保留系统-库相互作用中驱动诱导的频率移动，基于旋转后的系统缀饰基给出驱动辅助非相干跃迁的统一描述。应用于Kerr谐振子与非线性Jaynes-Cummings模型，并采用低能态近似进行分析。

Result: 发现驱动输入能量流存在显著的多光子共振增强，双光子驱动下峰值明显高于单光子情形，并解析给出双光子共振关系；非线性Jaynes-Cummings模型的共振结构非线性依赖于量子比特-光子耦合并出现额外缀饰态分支。低能态近似将共振电流增强归因于缀饰态杂化与非相干能量交换过程的高效激活。双光子驱动还显著改变二阶电流涨落与高阶累积量，提高时间归一化信噪比；引入双光子损耗会增加非相干光子对交换通道并进一步重塑电流及其涨落。

Conclusion: 结果有助于深化对光子驱动与量子耗散协同调控非平衡能量输运与涨落的理解。

Abstract: Understanding nonequilibrium transport and fluctuations driven by nonclassical light in nonlinear quantum optical systems is challenging. Here, we formulate a driven quantum master equation in the rotating frame combined with full counting statistics, retaining the drive-induced frequency shifts in the system-reservoir interactions and providing a unified description of drive-assisted incoherent transitions based on the rotated system dressed-basis. Applied to a Kerr resonator and a nonlinear Jaynes-Cummings model, the approach reveals pronounced multiphoton-resonant enhancement of the drive input energy current, with significant high peaks under two-photon driving compared to the single-photon case. The two-photon resonance relations are analytically obtained. Resonance structure in the nonlinear Jaynes-Cummings model nonlinearly relies on qubit-photon couplings, with additional dressed-state branches. A low-energy-state approximation attributes the resonant current enhancement to dressed-state hybridization and efficient activation of incoherent energy exchange processes. Beyond the average current, two-photon-driving induced energy exchange picture near resonance also substantially modifies the second-order current fluctuation and higher-order current cumulant, while increasing the time-normalized signal-to-noise ratio. Adding two-photon loss introduces extra incoherent photon-pair exchange pathways and further reshapes the current and its fluctuations. We hope these results may deepen interpretation of photon driving and quantum dissipation cooperatively governing nonequilibrium energy transport and fluctuations.

</details>


### [119] [QAOA-Based Pilot Assignment for Cell-Free Massive MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23657)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Fang Fang,Xianbin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将无蜂窝大规模MIMO中的导频分配问题重构为量子兼容的优化问题，采用QAOA直接求解，仿真表明其性能接近穷举搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无蜂窝大规模MIMO系统中导频污染是主要挑战，正交导频数量有限导致导频复用不可避免。由于导频分配解空间随用户数指数增长，经典计算机上求全局最优计算困难。量子计算的最新进展为求解此类大规模组合优化问题提供了有前景的途径。

Method: 将导频分配问题重构为量子兼容的优化问题，使其可由量子近似优化算法（QAOA）直接求解。具体而言，将导频污染目标函数与分配约束一并纳入QAOA的公式化表述中，从而使量子搜索聚焦于污染代价低的合法导频分配。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法的性能接近穷举搜索。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了量子辅助优化在未来无线系统中用于导频分配的潜力。

Abstract: Pilot contamination is a major challenge in cell-free massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems, where the limited number of orthogonal pilots makes pilot reuse unavoidable. Since the pilot assignment solution space grows exponentially with the number of users, finding the global optimum becomes computationally challenging on classical computers. Recent advances in quantum computing provide a promising approach for solving such large-scale combinatorial optimization problems. In this paper, we reformulate the assignment problem as a quantum-compatible optimization problem, enabling it to be directly solved by the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA). Specifically, the pilot contamination objective and assignment constraints are incorporated into the QAOA formulation. This allows the quantum search to focus on valid pilot assignments with low contamination cost. Simulation results show that the proposed method achieves performance close to exhaustive search, demonstrating the potential of quantum-assisted optimization for pilot assignment in future wireless systems.

</details>


### [120] [QuWARP: A Workload-Aware Reuse Planner for simulating Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23664)
*Tim Littau,Rihan Hai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QuWARP 是一种基于规划器的工作负载优化器，面向有界状态量子模拟器执行面，通过识别相关任务间的共享前缀并跨任务复用中间态（typed boundary artifacts），在 statevector 与 stabilizer-hybrid 模式下实现重复运行模拟加速，相比逐任务 Qrack 基线获得 2.95x–32.84x 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子线路模拟在实际中多为重复运行型工作负载（如 VQE 参数扫描、含噪多次采样、QEC 循环），不同运行间结构高度相关。现有模拟器虽优化单次执行，但忽视跨任务共享状态，导致大量可安全复用的计算被重复执行。

Method: 提出 QuWARP：在工作负载层面进行规划，识别跨相关任务的共享前缀，并决策何时将精确的类型化边界产物（typed boundary artifacts）物化以供后续复用；覆盖 statevector 模式与 stabilizer-hybrid 模式。其规划器以“续接合法性”作为严格正确性护栏，在复用不划算时主动放弃（abstain），并通过 EXPLAIN 风格的审计轨迹记录复用、放弃或拒绝决策，附带溯源信息与实际开销摘要。

Result: 在真实量子应用工作负载上，针对可复用（reuse-positive）负载，QuWARP 相对本文主要逐任务 Qrack 基线取得 2.95x–32.84x 的加速。

Conclusion: 工作负载级别的复用规划可有效提升重复运行型量子模拟的性能，同时通过可审计机制保证不支持的交接（handoffs）被隔离于执行路径之外，兼具正确性与可解释性。

Abstract: Quantum circuit simulation often appears as repeated-run workloads rather than isolated circuits: variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) sweeps, noisy multishot studies, and quantum error correction (QEC) cycles revisit closely related structure across many runs. Existing simulators optimise individual executions well, but they largely ignore cross-task shared state and therefore repeat work that could be reused safely. We propose QuWARP, a planner-based workload optimiser for a bounded state of the art simulator execution surface: it performs workload-level planning over related tasks, identifies shared prefixes, and chooses when to materialize exact typed boundary artifacts for later reuse across the evaluated statevector mode, and stabilizer-hybrid mode. Its planner treats continuation legality as a narrow correctness guardrail, abstains when reuse is unprofitable, and keeps each reuse, abstention, or refusal decision auditable through EXPLAIN-style traces, meaning inspectable planner reports with provenance and realized-cost summaries. Across real world quantum application workloads QuWARP delivers 2.95x-32.84x speedups over this work's main direct per-task Qrack denominator on reuse-positive workloads. These results show workload-level reuse planning improves repeated-run simulation while keeping unsupported handoffs auditable and out of the execution path.

</details>


### [121] [Non-Hermitian quantum phase transitions in the XY model induced by staggered imaginary Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23721)
*Hong Jiang,Xiang-Ping Jiang,Yan-Chao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了交错虚DM相互作用驱动的非厄米量子相变, 精确求解并揭示了RT对称破缺相, 提出新相干度量以刻画相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子信息度量(纠缠熵、量子失协、量子相干)无法捕捉RT对称破缺相变, 需要引入包含非厄米左右本征态信息的新探针。

Method: 在横向场XY链中引入交错虚DM相互作用; 通过交错非幺正变换精确对角化哈密顿量; 将D<1映射到标准厄米XY链解析相界; 分析D=1的奇异边界与D>1的RT破缺相; 提出基于RT对称与复共轭本征对关联的相干度量。

Result: D<1时与厄米XY链等价并得到完整相界; D=1为具有合并准粒子本征值与本征矢量的奇异边界; D>1为RT对称破缺相并含两个z铁磁相; 常用度量失效, 而新度量可靠识别RT破缺并准确分辨所有磁相界。

Conclusion: 交错虚DM相互作用可驱动非厄米量子相变, 新相干度量嵌入非厄米信息, 能有效表征RT对称破缺及磁性相界。

Abstract: We investigate non-Hermitian quantum phase transitions driven by staggered imaginary Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya (DM) interactions in a transverse-field $XY$ chain. By virtue of a staggered nonunitary transformation, we exactly diagonalize the Hamiltonian. For $D<1$, this procedure maps the non-Hermitian model onto a standard Hermitian $XY$ chain, allowing analytical derivation of the full phase boundaries. The parameter line $D=1$ forms an exceptional boundary with coalesced quasiparticle eigenvalues and eigenvectors, while the entire $D>1$ regime falls into the $\mathcal{RT}$-symmetry-broken phase containing two distinct $z$-ferromagnetic phases. We examine common quantum-information probes and show that conventional measures, such as entanglement entropy, quantum discord, and quantum coherence, fail to capture the $\mathcal{RT}$ symmetry-breaking transition. To address this deficiency, we propose a novel coherence measure $\widetilde{\mathrm{QC}}_{\max}^{\rm LR}$ based on $\mathcal{RT}$ symmetry and complex-conjugate eigenpair correlations. Embedding intrinsic non-Hermitian information of left and right eigenstates, $\widetilde{\mathrm{QC}}_{\max}^{\rm LR}$ reliably identifies the $\mathcal{RT}$ symmetry-breaking transition and accurately resolves all magnetic phase boundaries.

</details>


### [122] [Near-optimal incoherent tomography of low-rank quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23752)
*Kean Chen,Aadil Oufkir*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无量子记忆自适应实验下的量子信道层析，给出钻石范数误差ε下的最优及近最优查询复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化输入维度d1、输出维度d2、Kraus秩≤r的量子信道在钻石范数误差ε下的层析查询复杂度，并厘清自适应无量子记忆协议的能力极限。

Method: 对非零Choi特征值下有界Ω(d1/r)的信道，采用非自适应算法结合Surawy-Stepney等(2022)的估计量并给出新的钻石范数分析；下界通过构造局部信道族与单次查询Fisher信息一致界得到。对一般信道，将上述非自适应算法与矩阵乘法权重更新(MMWU)算法结合，在O(log(2d1))轮内自适应调整输入态。

Result: 良条件信道：查询上、下界均为Θ(d1d2r²/ε²)，上界由非自适应算法达到，下界适用于任意自适应非相干协议。一般信道：上界为O(d1d2r²log(2d1)/ε²)，与下界Ω(d1d2r²/ε²)近乎匹配。

Conclusion: 在无量子记忆约束下，非零Choi特征值有下界的信道层析查询复杂度达到最优Θ(d1d2r²/ε²)；一般信道仅需O(log(2d1))轮MMWU自适应即可将上界提升至与下界仅差对数因子。

Abstract: We study tomography for quantum channels with input dimension $d_1$, output dimension $d_2$, and Kraus rank at most $r$, to within diamond norm error $\varepsilon$, using adaptive experiments that retain no quantum memory between channel queries.
  - For quantum channels whose non-zero Choi eigenvalues are bounded below by $Ω(d_1/r)$, we establish optimal query upper and lower bounds $Θ(d_1d_2r^2/ε^2)$. The upper bound is achieved by a nonadaptive algorithm that uses the estimator from [Surawy-Stepney et al., Quantum (2022)], together with a new diamond-norm analysis. The lower bound applies to arbitrary adaptive incoherent protocols and follows from a new local family of channels and a uniform one-query Fisher-information bound.
  - For general channels, we establish an upper bound $O(d_1d_2r^2\log(2d_1)/ε^2)$, nearly matching the above lower bound $Ω(d_1d_2r^2/\varepsilon^2)$. To achieve this, we generalize the above nonadaptive algorithm by adapting the input state over $O(\log(2d_1))$ rounds with the Matrix Multiplicative Weight Update algorithm.

</details>


### [123] [Flow-Based Lattice Surgery Optimization with Runtime T Gate Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23756)
*Raymond Iacobacci,Tianyi Hao,Neer Patel,Siyuan Niu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: FlowRouter 是首个将 Clifford 路由与随机魔态培养统一到单一 3D 嵌入中的拓扑晶格手术编译器，通过最大流同时嵌入整层门并吸收培养随机性，显著降低时空体积并提升编译速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶格手术是表面码容错量子计算的主要路径，编译逻辑电路到晶格手术操作决定计算资源开销。现有编译器基于理想化假设：或不可扩展，或在层内逐门路由（嵌入依赖门的顺序，丧失批量路由机会），并假定魔态始终可用且无成本，但实际制备一个魔态比 Clifford 操作代价更高。因此需要面向实际场景的编译器。

Method: FlowRouter 将 Clifford 路由与随机魔态培养置于单一 3D 嵌入中。编译时，把整个电路层的路由建模为最大流问题，同时嵌入多个门；运行时，允许每个空闲 patch 培养魔态，支持带多路复用和多阶段后选择的真实培养过程，并通过延迟机制以尽可能小的时空体积开销吸收随机性。

Result: 在假定魔态免费时，相比最先进的静态拓扑晶格手术编译器，FlowRouter 将时空体积降低 2.3 倍，编译速度提升 10.8 倍。在同时计入 Clifford 与魔态成本、感知培养的设置下，在 distance-13 表面码上相比最先进的运行时编译器将时空体积降低 2.3 倍。

Conclusion: FlowRouter 首次在实际条件下统一处理 Clifford 路由与随机魔态培养，通过整层最大流嵌入与随机性吸收机制，在时空体积和编译速度上均优于现有最优方案，为面向实际场景的拓扑晶格手术编译提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Lattice surgery on the surface code is a leading route to fault-tolerant quantum computation. Compiling logical circuits into lattice surgery operations is a pivotal step that determines the resource cost of a computation. Prior compilers build on idealized assumptions, and one that works in practical settings remains to be developed. Existing works are either unscalable or route one gate at a time within a layer, so the embedding depends on the order of gates and opportunities to route many gates at once are lost. In addition, they assume magic states are always available and cost-free, even though preparing one costs more than a Clifford operation.
  We present FlowRouter, the first topological lattice surgery compiler that places Clifford routing and stochastic magic state cultivation inside a single 3D embedding. At compile time, FlowRouter formulates the routing of an entire circuit layer as a maximum-flow problem, embedding many gates simultaneously. At runtime, it allows magic states to be cultivated at every idling patch, works with the realistic cultivation process with multiplexing and multi-stage post-selections, and absorbs the stochasticity by introducing delay mechanisms that add as little spacetime volume as possible. With magic states assumed free, FlowRouter reduces spacetime volume by 2.3x and compiles 10.8x faster than the state-of-the-art static topological lattice surgery compiler. With the cultivation-aware setting that includes both Clifford and magic state cost, FlowRouter reduces spacetime volume by 2.3x on a distance-13 surface code when compared with the state-of-the-art runtime compiler.

</details>


### [124] [A four-state quantum communication protocol with mesoscopic twin beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23818)
*Stefano Carsi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将基于双光束(TWB)态的量子通信协议从二态扩展为四态方案，每轮可传输2比特；利用均值光子数与噪声约化因子(NRF)在(m,R)平面定义四个可区分态，并通过实验验证其性能与窃听检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TWB态具有强光子数关联，在介观区域可用SiPM等光子数分辨探测器直接观测，其非经典性由NRF量化，可用于量子关联见证和信道安全监测。原始TWB协议仅支持二进制编码，信息容量有限，需要扩展以提高每轮传输比特数。

Method: 将原二进制编码协议扩展为四态方案：在一臂上叠加两个均值光子数值和两个热噪声模式数，在(m,R)平面定义四个可区分态。均值探测光子数恢复第一比特，NRF区分第二比特并提供对截获-重发攻击的安全检查。通过两轮实验测量：先表征TWB源的光子数统计、NRF和探测效率以确定信道工作条件；再实现四态协议，分析态分布及95%置信区域、误差概率随样本量的变化，并比较机器学习分类器的态判别性能；最后考察NRF对截获-重发攻击的响应。

Result: 通过态分布与95%置信区域分析、误差概率随样本量变化及机器学习分类器对比评估了四态协议的性能；并研究了截获-重发攻击下NRF的响应，以评估协议检测窃听的能力。

Conclusion: TWB态的四态协议可在每轮传输2比特，NRF既用于第二比特判别又作为安全检测手段；实验证明该协议结合机器学习分类器可实现态区分，并能通过NRF变化检测截获-重发窃听。

Abstract: Twin-beam (TWB) states generated by parametric down-conversion exhibit strong photon-number correlations that can be exploited for quantum communication. In the mesoscopic regime, these correlations can be directly investigated using photon-number-resolving detectors, such as silicon photomultipliers (SiPMs). Their nonclassical nature is quantified by the noise reduction factor (NRF), which provides a witness of quantum correlations and a means of monitoring channel security. In this work, a quantum communication protocol based on TWB states, originally proposed for binary encoding, is extended to a four-state scheme capable of transmitting two bits per use. Information is encoded by combining two mean photon-number values and two mode numbers of thermal noise superimposed on one arm of the twin beam, defining four distinguishable states in the (m,R) plane. While the mean detected photon number enables the retrieval of one bit, the NRF provides discrimination of the second bit and a security check against intercept-and-resend attacks, which introduce additional noise and degrade the quantum correlations. The protocol is investigated through two experimental measurement campaigns. First, the twin-beam source is characterized in terms of photon-number statistics, NRF, and detection efficiency to establish the operating conditions of the communication channel. Subsequently, the four-state protocol is implemented and evaluated through the analysis of state distributions and their 95% confidence regions, error probability as a function of sample size, and a comparative assessment of machine learning classifiers for state discrimination. Finally, the response of the NRF to an intercept-and-resend attack is investigated to assess the protocol's ability to detect eavesdropping.

</details>


### [125] [Hidden collision statistics in bosonic heat transport: Superthermal correlations at fixed mean current](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23870)
*Iu. A. Nosal,A. E. Teretenkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 具有相同平均弛豫率的库在平均能量输运层面不可区分，但涨落存在差异。本文研究玻色模式经有限分束器碰撞与冷热泊松热辅助流耦合，平均演化为有限高斯事件信道生成的复合泊松半群，稳态为随机仿射不动点决定的热态混合，均占据数与平均热流与连续Lindblad库一致，高�s阶关联保留碰撞强度特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均弛豫率相同的热库可能在平均能量输运上难以区分，但涨落信息可能区分它们；作者希望刻画有限强度碰撞（非弱耦合极限）下碰撞型热库的非平衡统计与涨落性质，并厘清碰撞强度、温度对比对高阶关联与热流累积量的影响。

Method: 构建玻色模式与冷热热辅助态泊松流经有限分束器碰撞耦合的模型；推导平均演化为复合泊松半群并由有限高斯事件信道生成；用随机仿射不动点描述非平衡稳态热态混合；解析推导等透射率下的超热聚束律及前两阶热累积量渐近速率，分离局部单碰撞贡献与时间关联；用Fock空间对角化与蒙特卡洛轨迹数值验证。

Result: 非平衡稳态是热态精确混合，由随机仿射不动点决定；平均占据数动力学与热库分辨平均热流与匹配的连续Lindblad库完全一致，但高阶关联保留碰撞强度信息。等碰撞透射率下得到由温度对比和碰撞强度控制的精确超热聚束律。获得理想稳态事件分辨两点测量记录下前两阶热累积量的渐近速率，并区分局部单碰撞与时间关联贡献。Fock空间对角化与蒙特卡洛轨迹验证公式，并连接完全重置与弱碰撞高斯极限。

Conclusion: 碰撞型热库在平均能量输运层面可与连续Lindblad库等价，但涨落与高阶关联保留碰撞强度特征；由此可给出超热聚束律及热流累积量渐近速率，为区分相同平均弛豫率热库提供涨落层面的判据，并统一完全重置与弱碰撞高斯两个极限。

Abstract: Reservoirs with the same mean relaxation rate can be indistinguishable at the level of average energy transport while producing different fluctuations. We study a bosonic mode coupled to hot and cold Poisson streams of thermal ancillas through finite beam-splitter collisions. The averaged evolution is a compound-Poisson semigroup generated by finite Gaussian event channels. Its nonequilibrium steady state is an exact mixture of thermal states governed by a random affine fixed point. Consequently, the mean-occupation dynamics and bath-resolved mean heat currents coincide with those of the matched continuous Lindblad reservoir, whereas higher correlations retain the collision strength. For equal collision transmissivities, we derive an exact superthermal bunching law controlled by the temperature contrast and collision strength. We also obtain the asymptotic rates of the first two heat cumulants for an ideal stationary event-resolved two-point-measurement record and separate local one-collision contributions from temporal correlations. Fock-space diagonalization and Monte Carlo trajectories validate the formulas and connect full resets to the weak-collision Gaussian limit.

</details>


### [126] [Bell-certified retardance polarimetry from CHSH correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23914)
*J. Sumaya-Martinez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明Bell-CHSH测量中的符合计数数据除作为非局域性见证外，还可作为光学延迟相位传感的定量计量学凭证：从CHSH关联子导出经典Fisher信息下界，并给出免层析的置信下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bell-CHSH测量通常仅被视为非局域性检验工具，其数据所蕴含的计量学价值往往被忽略；作者希望同一套标准Bell实验数据能同时服务于非局域性基准与量子偏振测量的灵敏度认证。

Method: 针对单光子上的局域双折射相位，直接从实验可测的CHSH关联子推导经典Fisher信息，并用测量关联及CHSH参量对相位的导数给出其下界；在无偏边缘（unbiased-marginal）两比特情形下分析标准CHSH分析器设置的Bell最优性与Fisher最优性；进一步考察可见度下降、相位噪声、分析器失配与有限符合计数，构建保守的低置信界，无需量子态层析。

Result: 在无偏边缘两比特区域，标准CHSH分析器设置同时达到Bell最优和Fisher最优，在理想情形下饱和延迟估计的量子Fisher信息；所构造的保守置信下界可在存在噪声与有限计数时评估；且非零Fisher信息可在CHSH违背阈值以下持续存在，表明Bell违背与计量有用性是两种不同的资源。

Conclusion: 标准Bell检验数据集可同时提供非局域性基准和带统计控制的偏振测量灵敏度凭证，为量子偏振测量提供了一种无需层析的计量学方案。

Abstract: Bell-CHSH measurements with polarization-entangled photons are commonly used as nonlocality witnesses. Here we show that the same coincidence data can also provide a quantitative metrological certificate for optical retardance sensing. For a local birefringent phase encoded on one photon, we derive the classical Fisher information directly from experimentally accessible CHSH correlators and obtain a lower bound expressed in terms of the measured correlations and the phase derivative of the CHSH parameter. In the unbiased-marginal two-qubit regime, the canonical CHSH analyzer settings are shown to be simultaneously Bell-optimal and Fisher-optimal, saturating the quantum Fisher information for retardance estimation in the ideal case. We further analyze reduced visibility, phase noise, analyzer misalignment, and finite coincidence counts, and construct conservative lower confidence bounds that can be evaluated without quantum-state tomography. The results also clarify that Bell violation and metrological usefulness are distinct resources: nonzero Fisher information can persist below the CHSH-violation threshold. The protocol therefore enables a standard Bell-test data set to provide both a nonlocality benchmark and a statistically controlled sensitivity certificate for quantum polarimetry.

</details>


### [127] [Lee-Yang theorem for fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23942)
*Chaithanya Rayudu,Takahiro Misawa,Andrew Zhao,Jun Takahashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了广泛相互作用费米子模型配分函数的Lee-Yang零点自由定理，据此给出估计基态能量的高效量子算法，并严格确立了非零局域外场下这些模型不存在相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lee-Yang定理是研究多体系统的有力工具，可用于分析相变以及证明某些经典与量子算法的效率。然而对于相互作用费米子模型，尚缺乏一般性的零点自由结论，因而也难以据此构造可证明高效的量子算法。

Method: 证明了一类广泛相互作用费米子模型配分函数的Lee-Yang零点自由定理，并基于该零点自由性构造估计基态能量的量子算法，涵盖吸引Hubbard模型、二分图上的排斥Hubbard模型以及相互作用Hofstadter模型等。

Result: 所覆盖模型存在可证明高效的基态能量估计量子算法；同时严格证明了在存在非零局域外场时这些模型不发生相变。

Conclusion: 该工作将Lee-Yang零点自由性推广到广泛相互作用费米子模型，为量子算法效率提供严格保证，并给出相关模型相变不存在的严格结论。

Abstract: Lee-Yang theorems are a powerful tool for studying many-body systems, with applications ranging from analyzing phase transitions to proving the efficiency of certain classical and quantum algorithms. In this work, we prove a Lee-Yang zero-freeness theorem for the partition function of a broad class of interacting fermion models, implying the existence of a provably efficient quantum algorithm for estimating their ground-state energies. This class includes several well-known models such as the attractive Hubbard model, repulsive Hubbard model on bipartite graphs, and the interacting Hofstadter model. Our results also rigorously establish the nonexistence of phase transitions in these models in the presence of a nonzero local external field.

</details>


### [128] [A constructive violation of additivity of minimum output von Neumann entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23946)
*Laura Shou,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了最小输出冯诺依曼熵非可加性的一个显式非随机反例。证明采用有限维构造，模仿自由Haar酉行为；同一信道同时对任意1≤p≤∞的最小输出Rényi-p熵给出可加性违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小输出熵的可加性问题（additivity conjecture）是量子信息论中的核心问题，早期反例多依赖随机方法或概率论证。作者希望给出一个显式、非随机的反例，以更清晰地揭示非可加性的机制，并推广到更广的Rényi熵族。

Method: 构造一个有限维量子信道，其行为模仿自由概率论中的Haar酉随机矩阵（free Haar unitary）特性，从而在无随机性的前提下实现所需的谱性质，用以证明最小输出冯诺依曼熵的非可加性。

Result: 该显式信道对最小输出冯诺依曼熵呈现非可加性；同一信道对任意1≤p≤∞的最小输出Rényi-p熵也违反可加性。

Conclusion: 非可加性可以在一个确定性、有限维构造中实现，且该现象覆盖整个Rényi-p熵范围，表明最小输出熵的可加性失效具有普遍性而非随机偶然现象。

Abstract: We give an explicit non-random example of nonadditivity of minimum output von Neumann entropy. The proof uses a finite-dimensional construction which imitates free Haar unitary behavior. The same channel also gives a violation of additivity for the minimum output Rényi-$p$ entropy for any $1\le p\le\infty$.

</details>


### [129] [Global zero-excitation state preparation through subsystem cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23960)
*Kerstin Beer,Daniel Burgarth*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究局域耗散驱动激发数守恒量子系统弛豫至唯一全局吸引零激发态的条件，证明当耗散子系统构成零强制集时零激发态是唯一全局吸引稳态，可作为图论判据实现基态冷却。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟与量子信息处理中，将相互作用量子系统制备到低能或基态是基本任务，但实际体系中耗散与主动冷却通常只能施加在有限子系统上，缺乏局域耗散实现全局态制备的判据。

Method: 研究由GKLS主方程描述、含局域跳跃算符的激发数守恒系统耗散动力学；利用哈密顿量激发转移对应的图结构，结合零强制集概念，建立局域耗散驱动全系统至唯一全局吸引零激发态的充分条件，并在最近邻Heisenberg自旋链上验证，进一步约化到单激发子空间进行标度估计。

Result: 证明若激发转移图使耗散子系统为零强制集，则零激发态是唯一全局吸引稳态；当该态与哈密顿量基态重合时实现基态冷却；在最近邻Heisenberg链上给出随链长的标度估计，表明冷却高效。

Conclusion: 为局域耗散实现全局量子态制备提供了图论判据，并说明局域冷却可高效实现基态制备。

Abstract: Preparing interacting quantum systems in low-energy or ground states is a fundamental task in quantum simulation and quantum information processing. In realistic settings, dissipation and active cooling can typically be engineered only on a limited subset of the system. We study the dissipative dynamics of excitation-number conserving quantum systems governed by a GKLS master equation with local jump operators acting on a subset of qubits. We establish sufficient conditions under which such localized dissipation drives the full system to a unique globally attractive zero-excitation state. In particular, we prove that if the Hamiltonian generates excitation transfer described by a graph for which the dissipative subsystem forms a zero forcing set, then the zero-excitation state is the unique globally attractive stationary state. When this state coincides with a ground state of the Hamiltonian, the same mechanism realizes ground-state cooling. Our results provide a graph-theoretic criterion for global state preparation from localized dissipation, which we illustrate using a nearest-neighbor Heisenberg spin chain. For this model, a reduction to the single-excitation sector further yields a scaling estimate with the length of the chain which indicates efficient cooling.

</details>


### [130] [Trace-distance-based complementarity relations in a multipath interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24074)
*Yue Sun,Jingyan Liu,Peng-Tong Li,Chenxu Li,Ming-Jing Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用迹距离量化多路径干涉仪中的波动性与粒子性，并建立相应的二象性与三象性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 干涉仪中的互补性关系体现了量子力学的波粒二象性这一重要现象，需要对其中的波动行为和粒子行为进行量化研究。

Method: 发展一种量化多路径干涉仪中波动性与粒子性的方法，发现迹距离（trace distance）是波动性和粒子性度量的合适候选量。

Result: 基于迹距离分别建立了一些二象性关系（duality relations）和三象性关系（triality relations）。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅将迹距离的应用拓展到干涉仪中，也为波动性与粒子性的量化提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The complementarity relations in an interferometer reflect an important phenomenon in quantum mechanics: wave-particle duality. Here, we develop a method to quantify both wave and particle behaviors in a multi-path interferometer. In particular, we find that the trace distance is a good candidate for wave and particle measures. As a result, some duality relations and triality ralations are established respectively. This work not only extends the application of the trace distance to the interferometer, but also opens up new perspectives on the quantification of waveness and particleness.

</details>


### [131] [Pauli-resolved virtual distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24132)
*Si-Yuan Chen,Congcong Zheng,Kun Wang,Ming-Cheng Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅需单一2m副本测量设置即可学习虚拟蒸馏态全部4^n个Pauli矩平方的方法，样本复杂度为O(m[n+log(1/δ)]/ε²)，相比此前协议在系统尺寸n上实现指数级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习虚拟蒸馏量子态ρ^m/tr(ρ^m)的完整Pauli profile此前需要指数级数量的ρ副本，如何突破这一指数瓶颈是亟待解决的问题。

Method: 利用配对副本使Pauli算符的反对易符号相消，从而"塌缩"Pauli族的不兼容性；引入量子Bernstein范数作为可计算的非线性泛函不兼容性度量来验证这种塌缩；对m=2提出相干Bell差分采样电路以在现有设备上实现该测量；并进一步给出信息论层面的m副本协议。

Result: 所有4^n个Pauli矩平方[tr(Pρ^m)]²可用O(m[n+log(1/δ)]/ε²)份副本以加性误差ε、置信度1-δ学习；信息论协议以O(m[n+log(1/δ)]/ε⁴)份副本恢复带符号矩tr(Pρ^m)；该协议在给定下界区间内达到最优副本数。

Conclusion: 通过在固定m下实现最优副本数，且任何更少副本的协议都需指数级副本，工作确立了单一测量设置下虚拟蒸馏态Pauli profile学习的指数加速与副本最优性。

Abstract: Learning the full Pauli profile of the virtually distilled quantum state $ρ^m/\text{tr}(ρ^m)$ has so far required exponentially many copies of $ρ$. We show that all $4^n$ squared Pauli moments $[\text{tr}(Pρ^m)]^2$ can be learned to additive error $\varepsilon$ with confidence $1-δ$ from one $2m$-replica measurement setting using $O(m[n+\log(1/δ)]/\varepsilon^2)$ copies. This is an exponential speedup in the system size $n$ over previous protocols. For $m = 2$, we propose the coherent Bell difference sampling circuit that realizes this measurement on current devices. The speedup originates from paired replicas that cancel the anticommutation signs of Pauli operators, collapsing the incompatibility of the Pauli family. We certify this collapse by introducing the quantum Bernstein norm, a computable incompatibility measure for nonlinear functionals. A further information-theoretic $m$-replica protocol recovers the signed moments $\text{tr}(Pρ^m)$ using $O(m[n+\log(1/δ)]/\varepsilon^4)$ copies. For fixed $m$ in the stated lower-bound regime, it attains the optimal replica number, since any protocol with fewer replicas requires exponentially many copies.

</details>


### [132] [Sparse and weak-measurement certification of graph-edge entanglement in PXP scar wavepackets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24139)
*Ximo Wang,Yichi Zhang,Qiwei Han,Xi Zhao,Yuhang Wang,Chunxiao Du,Rui Li,Wenxiu Li,Hao Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种有限记录数协议，用于在图修饰PXP链的scar波包上认证可局域化纠缠（图边），无需精确本征态或暗态嵌入假设，并通过统计置信界与扰动测试验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不完美的多体复活本身不能证明返回态的纠缠；已有纠缠见证方法通常依赖精确目标本征态或暗态嵌入假设，而实际scar波包对应的目标簇态具有非零能量方差，因此需要一种不依赖这些强假设、可在有限采样与真实扰动下给出统计可靠结论的认证协议。

Method: 在graph-dressed PXP链上设计有限记录协议：针对未变形哈密顿量Pauli项的新鲜二值探针在修饰阻塞子空间内具有完全可分离解释，需加入局域图稳定子探针以获得纠缠见证；采用平方根仪器、最坏情况扰动预算确定强度，最小化等分配Hoeffding充分采样成本；用局域对易子界处理哈密顿量活跃期间有限时长ancilla脉冲的影响。

Result: 在至多二十自旋的链、扰动动力学及独立实现上测试：在首个二十自旋返回处，合成弱记录认证了全部十九条图边；生成元、双色、有界权重与全群基准将这一可局域化资源与真正的多体认证区分开来。

Conclusion: 该协议可在已知scar波包中测量可恢复纠缠，给出显式校准及对其物理与统计解释的限制，同时避免将其误判为真正的多体纠缠认证。

Abstract: An imperfect many-body revival does not by itself certify the entanglement of the returning state. We give a finite-record protocol for graph-edge localizable entanglement along scar wavepackets of a graph-dressed PXP chain. The target cluster state has nonzero energy variance, so neither an exact target eigenstate nor a dark-state embedding is assumed. Fresh binary probes of the undeformed Hamiltonians Pauli terms have a fully separable explanation, even within the dressed blockade sector. Adding local graph-stabilizer probes makes established entanglement witnesses accessible with simultaneous confidence bounds. For a specified square-root instrument, an imposed worst-case disturbance budget fixes the strength that minimizes the equal-allocation Hoeffding sufficient sampling cost. A local commutator bound accounts for finite-duration ancilla pulses while the Hamiltonian remains active. We test the protocol on chains through twenty spins, under perturbed dynamics, and with independent implementations. At the first twenty-spin return, synthetic weak records certify all nineteen graph edges. Generator, two-color, bounded-weight, and full-group benchmarks separate this localizable resource from genuine multipartite certification. The protocol measures recoverable entanglement in a known scar wavepacket, with explicit calibration and limits on its physical and statistical interpretation.

</details>


### [133] [Reconstructing the phonon distribution of trapped ions out of the Lamb-Dicke regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24147)
*Christophe H. Valahu,Prachi Nagpal,Teerawat Chalermpusitarak,Maverick J. Millican,Cameron McGarry,Frank Scuccimarra,Vassili G. Matsos,Hon-Kwan Chan,Ting Rei Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将声子态重构重新表述为滤波函数反演问题，利用载波与多边带复合脉冲设计声子空间中良态的滤波器，实现对纯态与混态的刻画、加热动力学研究，以及高达n=250的Fock态重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表征囚禁离子运动态的声子分布对量子信息处理诸多应用至关重要，但现有方法在Lamb-Dicke区之外变得病态（ill-conditioned），限制了高激发态与强耦合条件下的重构能力。

Method: 把声子态重构建模为滤波函数反演问题；在载波和多个边带上施加复合脉冲（composite pulses），从而在声子空间中构造出良态（well-conditioned）的滤波器。

Result: 可表征纯态和混态、研究加热动力学，并重构最高至n=250的Fock态。

Conclusion: 以滤波函数反演替代传统重构思路，并配合复合脉冲工程设计良态滤波器，突破了Lamb-Dicke区限制，为囚禁离子运动态的高激发态与混合态表征提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Characterizing the phonon distribution of trapped-ion motional states is essential for many applications in quantum information processing, but existing methods become ill-conditioned beyond the Lamb-Dicke regime. We overcome this limitation by recasting phonon-state reconstruction as a filter-function inversion problem. Using composite pulses on the carrier and multiple sidebands, we engineer well-conditioned filters in phonon space to characterize pure and mixed states, study heating dynamics, and reconstruct Fock states up to $n=250$.

</details>


### [134] [Nondemolition filtering of an embedded cluster-state scar under continuous local monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24166)
*Ximo Wang,Xi Zhao,Xiayu Sun,Qiwei Han,Yuhang Wang,Chunxiao Du,Wenxiu Li,Hao Zhang,Rui Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文构造了一个具有精确簇态本征矢的局域环状哈密顿量，并研究其稳定子缺陷在连续监测下的动力学，给出有限时间界与资源限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别多体谱中的低纠缠本征态与在测量过程中保持它是两个不同任务；本文旨在将二者统一到一个明确可验证的框架中。

Method: 构造显式局域环哈密顿量，使其具有精确的簇态本征矢；对任意混合输入连续监测稳定子缺陷；借助正缺陷算符间隙推导有限时间界，并用十一个量子比特内的精确对角化及独立矩阵与轨迹计算进行验证。

Result: 条件簇保真度为初始目标权重除以未观测到点击的概率；正缺陷算符间隙在非对易动力学、非正规有效生成元和不完美探测下给出有限时间界；固定总监测速率下保证指数随系统尺寸反比下降；高条件保真度不能消除由初始重叠决定的制备成本；数值证据显示混沌谱背景中存在簇态离群值，并给出保守的相干误差界。

Conclusion: 该构造专门化了已有的疤痕嵌入与无破坏验证框架，明确了测量假设、有限时间保证与资源限制，表明高保真度验证受初始重叠决定的制备成本制约。

Abstract: Identifying a low-entanglement eigenstate inside a many-body spectrum and preserving it during measurement are distinct tasks. We construct an explicit local ring Hamiltonian with an exact cluster-state eigenvector and study continuous monitoring of its stabilizer defects. For arbitrary mixed inputs, the conditional cluster fidelity is the initial target weight divided by the no-observed-click probability. A positive defect-operator gap gives finite-time bounds that hold for noncommuting Hamiltonian dynamics, nonnormal effective generators and imperfect detection. At fixed total monitoring rate, the guaranteed exponent falls inversely with system size; high conditional fidelity does not remove the preparation cost set by the initial overlap. Exact diagonalization up to eleven qubits gives finite-size evidence for a cluster-state outlier in a chaotic spectral background. Independent matrix and trajectory calculations verify the dynamics and a conservative coherent-error bound. This construction specializes established scar embedding and nondemolition verification frameworks, with explicit measurement assumptions, finite-time guarantees and resource limitations.

</details>


### [135] [No violation on a generalisation of Leggett-Garg inequality and Bell-CHSH inequality with extended probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24169)
*Sirawit Kajonsombat,Pongwit Srisangyingcharoen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将经典概率替换为扩展概率，提出Leggett-Garg不等式（LGI）与Bell-CHSH不等式的推广形式，并引入广义宏观实在论与广义局域实在论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典概率框架下LGI与Bell-CHSH不等式存在被量子系统违反的问题；作者试图通过引入比经典概率更底层的"扩展概率"来扩展这些不等式的边界，并在此框架下重新刻画宏观实在论与局域实在论。

Method: 以扩展概率替代经典概率；在一致历史（consistent history）框架下，将底层现实描述为"不可结算历史"（non-settleable history）；据此推导出推广的LGI与Bell-CHSH不等式，并在扩展概率框架内定义与分析广义宏观实在论和广义局域实在论，分析过程不依赖显式测量。

Result: 推广后的不等式在任意测量设置下均被满足而不被违反；广义宏观实在论与广义局域实在论可在不含显式测量的扩展概率框架内得到刻画。

Conclusion: 扩展概率为经典概率提供了更底层的现实层面，可在不违反不等式的前提下容纳量子行为，并为理解量子系统如何拥有经典性质提供了新途径。

Abstract: A generalisation of the Leggett-Garg inequality (LGI) and the Bell-CHSH inequality is proposed in this work, by replacing a classical probability notion with an extended probability notion. The extended probability serves as an underlying layer of reality to the classical probability. Within the consistent history framework, the underlying layer is so-called a non-settleable history. Hence, the bounds of the LGIs and Bell-CHSH inequalities are extended. The resulting generalised inequalities are satisfied without violation for arbitrary measurement settings. Furthermore, generalised Macro- realism and generalised Local realism are introduced and analysed within the extended probability framework without relying on an explicit measurement. This opens up further possibilities on one of the great pursuits in physics, understanding on how a quantum system can possess classical properties.

</details>


### [136] [A QSVT-Based Quantum Jacobi Algorithm for Linear Systems with Application to the Poisson Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24266)
*Louisa M. Piskol,Thorsten Grahs,Stefan Langer,Oleksandr Kyriienko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作基于量子奇异值变换（QSVT）实现Jacobi迭代法的量子版本，用于求解CFD离散化产生的大型稀疏线性系统，并在一维/二维Poisson问题及顶盖驱动方腔流的压力Poisson方程上进行了演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFD算法通过离散化求解偏微分方程，会产生大型稀疏线性系统；经典上多用迭代法求解，而现有量子线性求解器多针对矩阵求逆而非迭代逼近，缺乏与经典CFD求解策略对应的量子迭代方法。

Method: 将Jacobi迭代重写为对块编码算子（block-encoded operator）的多项式变换，借助量子奇异值变换（QSVT）实现该迭代过程。

Result: 该算法相对于迭代次数只需常数级别的辅助量子比特开销（ancilla overhead），电路深度随迭代次数线性增长；并在一维和二维Poisson问题（含Chorin投影法中顶盖驱动方腔流的压力Poisson方程）上完成了演示。

Conclusion: 该算法为未来多重网格方法与预处理技术的量子实现提供了有前景的基础模块，使量子算法更接近成熟的CFD求解策略。

Abstract: Many computational fluid dynamics (CFD) algorithms solve partial differential equations by discretization, resulting in large and sparse systems of linear equations. While iterative methods are widely used to solve these systems classically, most existing quantum linear system solvers target the solution through matrix inversion rather than approximating it using an iterative procedure. In this work, we develop a quantum implementation of the Jacobi method based on quantum singular value transformation (QSVT). By reformulating the Jacobi iteration as a polynomial transformation of a block-encoded operator, the algorithm requires only a constant ancilla overhead with respect to the number of iterations while maintaining a circuit depth that scales linearly with the iteration number. We demonstrate the algorithm for one- and two-dimensional Poisson problems, including the pressure Poisson equation arising in Chorin's projection method for the lid-driven cavity flow. The proposed algorithm provides a promising building block for future quantum implementations of multigrid methods and preconditioning techniques, bringing quantum algorithms closer to established CFD solution strategies.

</details>


### [137] [On the Born rule in a new quantum approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24269)
*Inge S. Helland*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在新量子基础研究框架下，通过若干步骤在弱条件下证明了玻恩规则，其唯一前提为"跃迁概率原理"，并由此得到比传统有限维量子力学更简单的态概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子力学中玻恩规则多作为独立公设引入，缺乏更基本的推导依据；作者希望在新量子基础路径下，以尽可能弱的假设重构其地位，从而获得更基础的态概念。

Method: 以"跃迁概率原理"作为唯一弱条件（该原理与统计学中的似然原理密切相关），通过多步骤推导证明玻恩规则，并从多个视角对该条件进行讨论。

Result: 在跃迁概率原理这一弱条件下成功导出玻恩规则；该条件与似然原理的关联得到阐明，并由此形成了较传统有限维量子力学更为简化的态概念。

Conclusion: 玻恩规则可在单一弱假设下被证明，表明其并非必须作为独立公设；该路径为量子基础提供了一种更简约的态描述框架。

Abstract: In the context of a new approach towards quantum foundation, the Born rule is proved under a weak condition through several steps. The sole weak condition behind the Born rule is called the transition probability principle, which is closely related to the likelihood principle of statistics. This condition is discussed from several points of view. The whole approach leads to a simpler state concept than the traditional one in finite-dimensional quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [138] [Quantum Computing Solution of the Bethe-Salpeter Equation for Relativistic Scalar Bound States via Tensor-Network VQE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24282)
*Gerhard Hellstern*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将齐次Bethe-Salpeter方程(hBSE)约化为对称矩阵本征值问题，用MPS张量网络变分量子本征求解器(VQE)求解，成功恢复最小耦合常数(误差<1%)，但分析表明该问题处于经典可解区，未展现量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索门基量子计算能否处理束缚态问题：hBSE是相对论束缚态的核心方程，期望借助量子算法获得超越经典方法的计算能力。

Method: 经Wick旋转到欧氏空间并作O(4) S波分波投影，将hBSE约化为维度N=2^n的对称矩阵本征值问题；把哈密顿量分解为n量子比特Pauli算符之和，采用矩阵乘积态(MPS)拟设的变分量子本征求解器(VQE)求解；并对纠缠结构、Pauli开销与梯度行为作批判性分析。

Result: N=16(n=4量子比特)时VQE恢复最大本征值(对应束缚的最小耦合常数)，平均相对误差优于1%；BSE振幅纠缠度低，近似满足面积律。

Conclusion: 三项障碍——指数级Pauli开销、近似尺寸无关的低纠缠、梯度尺度随n下降(暗示barren plateau)——表明该特定问题处于经典可解区，低键维张量网络与MPS/Lanczos方法即可高效处理。这是首次在量子比特编码下量化BSE振幅纠缠的负面结果，并指出2D Minkowski空间BSE、N体束缚态与非梯形核是可能实现真实量子优势的扩展方向。

Abstract: We present a gate-based quantum computing solution of the homogeneous Bethe-Salpeter equation (hBSE) for the bound state of two massive relativistic scalar particles interacting via ladder-approximation scalar exchange. After Wick rotation to Euclidean space and O(4) S-wave partial-wave projection, the hBSE is reduced to a symmetric matrix eigenvalue problem of dimension N = 2^n. We decompose the resulting Hamiltonian into a sum of n-qubit Pauli operators and solve it with the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) using a Matrix Product State (MPS) tensor-network ansatz. For N=16 (n=4 qubits), the VQE recovers the maximum eigenvalue - which encodes the minimum coupling constant for binding - to better than 1 % mean relative error compared to classical diagonalization. Entanglement analysis of the BSE amplitude shows low, area-law-like entanglement over the tested sizes, which motivates the MPS ansatz. A critical analysis of three independent barriers - exponential Pauli overhead, approximately size-independent low entanglement, and decreasing VQE gradient scales for the tested ansatz, consistent with generic barren-plateau concerns at larger n - reveals that the specific problem studied here lies in a classically tractable regime: over the tested range it is well described by low-bond-dimension tensor networks and efficiently handled by MPS/Lanczos methods. This negative result provides, to our knowledge, the first entanglement quantification of the BSE amplitude in qubit encoding, establishes the Pauli Hamiltonian framework for future BSE variants, and identifies 2D Minkowski-space BSE, N-body bound states, and non-ladder kernels as physically motivated extensions where genuine quantum advantage may become plausible.

</details>


### [139] [New lower bounds for CDS and $f$-routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24291)
*Atsuya Hasegawa,Ranitha Mataraarachchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究非局域量子计算(NLQC)中纠缠代价的下界问题，聚焦于f-routing这一核心特例。作者建立了两个相关下界：一是鲁棒CDS的共享随机性代价下界为确定性SMP通信复杂度的对数；二是一侧完美f-routing的纠缠代价可由符号秩给出一般下界，并证明内积函数的路由纠缠代价为线性下界，与已知上界匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局域量子计算(NLQC)的纠缠代价与复杂度理论、密码学、量子引力等领域密切相关，其核心特例f-routing受量子位置验证问题驱动。然而，在完全鲁棒(fully robust)设定下证明f-routing纠缠代价的下界一直是NLQC中的重大公开难题。本文受此问题驱动，旨在通过研究相关问题的下界来推进对该难题的理解。

Method: 方法上有两条路径。其一，研究鲁棒条件秘密披露(CDS)的共享随机性代价，利用Allerstorfer等人(Quantum 2024)建立的CDS与f-routing之间的联系，将鲁棒CDS的随机性复杂度作为通向完全鲁棒路由下界的自然步骤；通过证明鲁棒CDS的共享随机性代价下界为确定性SMP通信复杂度的对数(即使通信和私有随机性不受限制)。其二，考虑一侧完美(one-sided-perfect)f-routing(在一个输入类上精确、另一类上常数误差)，利用Asadi、Culf和May(ITCS 2025)方法中出现的低秩矩阵的正性(positivity)来推导基于符号秩(sign rank)的一般纠缠代价下界。

Result: 第一，证明了鲁棒CDS的共享随机性代价下界为确定性SMP通信复杂度的对数，即使通信与私有随机性不受限制也不受影响；该下界对相等(equality)函数是紧的。第二，通过低秩矩阵正性方法得到一侧完美f-routing纠缠代价关于符号秩的一般下界；特别地，对两个一侧完美设定下的内积(inner-product)函数路由，得到了线性的纠缠代价下界，且与已知上界相匹配。

Conclusion: 论文建立了两个与NLQC纠缠代价相关的新下界：鲁棒CDS的共享随机性下界（对相等函数紧）以及基于符号秩的一侧完美f-routing纠缠代价下界（对内积函数达到线性且紧）。这些结果为理解完全鲁棒f-routing的纠缠代价这一重大公开问题提供了重要进展。

Abstract: Understanding the entanglement cost of non-local quantum computation (NLQC) is relevant to complexity theory, cryptography, quantum gravity, and related areas. A central special case is $f$-routing, motivated in part by quantum position verification. Proving lower bounds on its entanglement cost in the fully robust setting has been a major open problem in NLQC.
  Motivated by this problem, we establish two related lower bounds. First, we study the shared-randomness cost of robust conditional disclosure of secrets (CDS). The connection between CDS and $f$-routing established by Allerstorfer et al. (Quantum 2024) makes understanding the randomness complexity of robust CDS a natural step toward lower bounds for the fully robust routing problem. We show that the shared-randomness cost of robust CDS is lower bounded by the logarithm of deterministic SMP communication complexity, even when communication and private randomness are unrestricted. Our lower bound is tight for the equality function.
  Second, we consider one-sided-perfect $f$-routing, in which the protocol is exact on one input class and has constant error on the other. By exploiting the positivity of the low-rank matrix arising in the method of Asadi, Culf, and May (ITCS 2025), we derive a general lower bound on the entanglement cost in terms of sign rank. In particular, this yields a linear lower bound on the entanglement cost of routing for the inner-product function in both one-sided-perfect settings, matching the known upper bound.

</details>


### [140] [A Sharp Noise Threshold for Shor's Quantum Factoring and Discrete Log Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24316)
*Jin-Yi Cai,Ben Young*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究Shor量子因数分解与离散对数算法在量子傅里叶变换中受控旋转门噪声影响下的渐近行为，确定了急剧且极小的噪声阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cai (2024)与Cai and Young (2025)已给出相关噪声分析结果，本文旨在改进这些结果并精确刻画噪声阈值。

Method: 分析Shor算法量子傅里叶变换中精确受控旋转门受噪声干扰时的渐近行为，刻画噪声阈值。

Result: 确定了急剧且极小的噪声阈值：低于该阈值时两算法在期望多项式时间内成功；高于该阈值且素因子属于某正密度集合时，算法可证地在期望多项式时间内失败。

Conclusion: Shor因数分解与离散对数算法对受控旋转门噪声存在急剧阈值现象，改进了既有结果。

Abstract: We study the asymptotic behavior of Shor's quantum factoring and discrete log algorithms when noise affects the precise controlled rotation gates in their quantum Fourier transforms. Improving the results of Cai (2024) and Cai and Young (2025), we identify a sharp and vanishingly small noise threshold. If the noise level lies below this threshold, then the two algorithms succeed in expected polynomial time. If the noise level exceeds this threshold, then the algorithms provably fail to solve their respective problems in expected polynomial time when the underlying primes belong to a set of positive density.

</details>


### [141] [Robust self-testing of nonmaximal entanglement from a reduced inner product game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24349)
*Ranyiliu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将 Lalonde 的伪瞬移内积博弈从六维降至五维，保持 Alice 四问题、Bob 三问题、答案字母表大小五，证明该博弈可自检验其非最大纠缠态与全部 35 个测量效应，并给出鲁棒性界及推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lalonde 伪瞬移内积博弈是量子自检验与非局域性研究的重要实例。降维可简化实验实现并揭示更本质的结构，同时需给出鲁棒自检验以保证物理可实现性。

Method: 构造五维版本：Alice 四个问题、Bob 三个问题、答案集大小五；通过自检验分析证明该博弈唯一（在局部等价意义下）确定非最大纠缠态及 35 个测量效应；采用提取信道下的最优平方保真度定义距离 d_ext，导出鲁棒界；再借助紧性论证给出测量作用的同步定性鲁棒性。

Result: 证明该约化博弈自检验非最大纠缠态及全部 35 个测量效应；鲁棒界为 d_ext ≤ min{1, 2^21 √ε}（当策略获胜概率≥1−ε）；并证明任何有限维最大纠缠态均无法完美获胜该博弈。

Conclusion: 该五维博弈构成一个紧凑且鲁棒的自检验实例，限制了最大纠缠态在此类内积博弈中的完美获胜能力，为自检验理论及其实验简化提供新工具。

Abstract: We reduce Lalonde's pseudo-telepathy inner product game from six to five dimensions, keeping its four Alice questions and three Bob questions and reducing each answer alphabet to five. We prove that the reduced game self-tests its nonmaximally entangled state and all 35 measurement effects. The self-test is also robust: for a strategy winning with probability at least $1-\varepsilon$ we obtain $d_{\mathrm{ext}}\le\min\{1,2^{21}\sqrt{\varepsilon}\}$, where $d_{\mathrm{ext}}=\sqrt{1-Ξ}$ and $Ξ$ is the optimal squared fidelity with the reference state under local extraction channels. A compactness argument then gives simultaneous qualitative robustness for all measurement actions on the state under common local isometries. As a consequence of the self-test, no maximally entangled state of any finite dimension can win the game perfectly.

</details>


### [142] [Versatile Quantum Machine Learning with an Ultra-low Power Photonic Quantum Reservoir Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24381)
*Wei Wang,Zan Tang,Menglong Fang,Daiqin Su,Mile Gu,Jayne Thompson,Lip Ket Chin,Hong Cai,Leong-Chuan Kwek,Ai-Qun Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种集成光子量子储备池计算芯片，无需对有源调谐储备池核心，即可实现非线性映射、衰减记忆与多任务通用性，并在零偏置（所有片上移相器不加电）状态下保持竞争性性能，将计算功耗降至零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成光子微处理器擅长高带宽、大规模并行线性计算，但在机器学习所需的非线性特征映射与时间记忆方面仍存在关键瓶颈；传统方案依赖有源调谐与额外非线性元件，导致架构复杂度与功耗开销增加。

Method: 构建集成光子量子储备池计算机：结合量子态编码、多模干涉混频与光子统计读出；储备池核心无需有源调谐，可在零偏置状态下运行。

Result: 同一芯片支持静态分类、动态预测与稳定的自主预测；在零偏置状态下仍保持竞争性性能，无需有源控制，计算功耗降为零。

Conclusion: 该被动运行架构为低功耗、大规模量子储备池计算提供了物理上自洽的范式，并展示了一条可扩展的多功能机器学习硬件路径：大规模光子量子处理器无需重构内部光网络即可被复用为储备池。

Abstract: Integrated photonic microprocessors provide high-bandwidth, massively parallel linear computation, but realizing nonlinear feature maps and temporal memory remain key challenges for machine learning. Conventional approaches rely on active tuning and additional nonlinear elements, increasing architectural complexity and power overhead. Here we demonstrate an integrated photonic quantum reservoir computer that achieves nonlinear mapping, fading memory, and task versatility without active tuning of the reservoir core. The same chip supports accurate static classification, dynamic prediction, and stable autonomous forecasting, establishing broad utility across both classification and temporal inference tasks. Competitive performance is retained in the zero-bias state, where all on-chip phase shifters are unpowered, eliminating active control and reducing computational power consumption to zero. This passive operation highlights a scalable route to multifunctional machine-learning hardware, where large-scale photonic quantum processors can be repurposed as reservoirs without reconfiguring their internal optical networks. By combining quantum-state encoding, multimode interferometric mixing, and photon-statistical readout, this architecture provides a physically grounded paradigm for low-power, large-scale quantum reservoir computing.

</details>


### [143] [Quantum Work Extraction via Conditional Spatial Displacements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24425)
*Necati Çelik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种借助连续变量指针的测量辅助反馈协议，从相干量子电池中提取功，并在二能级电池与高斯指针模型下证明其"daemonic ergotropy"等于标准ergotropy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池在相干态下具有可提取的功，但当其与辅助系统（指针）因相互作用纠缠后，若将指针自由度迹掉，部分可提取功会变得不可及。作者希望借助测量辅助反馈（量子麦克斯韦妖）机制，探索能否恢复这部分看似丢失的功，并厘清量子相干与测量在热力学中的作用。

Method: 协议基于幺正算符 U = exp(-i k t Ĥ ⊗ P̂/ħ)，在电池能量本征态与辅助指针位置之间建立纠缠；随后对指针位置作投影测量，条件性地将电池制备到纯态，再通过反馈幺正操作提取功。作者针对二能级量子电池与高斯指针具体计算条件态及相应的 daemonic ergotropy，并与标准 ergotropy 比较。

Result: 在所考虑的纯初态下，对于所有相互作用强度，daemonic ergotropy 均等于标准 ergotropy。这表明测量辅助反馈能够完全恢复因与指针纠缠、在指针自由度被迹掉时本会不可及的全部可提取功。

Conclusion: 该协议提供了量子麦克斯韦妖的一个物理上清晰的实现，其中指针作为量子测量辅助，其位置与电池能量相关联。方案适用于囚禁离子系统的实验实现，并有助于理解量子相干与测量在热力学中的作用。

Abstract: We propose a protocol for extracting work from a coherent quantum battery state by exploiting measurement-assisted feedback mediated by a continuous-variable pointer. The scheme relies on the unitary operator $U = \exp(-i k t \, \hat{H} \otimes \hat{P}/\hbar)$, which generates entanglement between the battery's energy eigenstates and the position of an auxiliary pointer. A subsequent projective measurement of the pointer's position conditionally prepares the battery in a pure state from which work can be extracted via a feedback unitary. We analyze the protocol for a two-level quantum battery and a Gaussian pointer, computing the conditional states and the corresponding daemonic ergotropy. For the pure initial state considered, we find that the daemonic ergotropy equals the standard ergotropy for all interaction strengths, demonstrating that the measurement-assisted feedback recovers the full extractable work that would otherwise become inaccessible due to entanglement with the pointer when its degrees of freedom are traced out. The protocol thus provides a physically transparent realization of a quantum Maxwell demon, where the pointer acts as a quantum measurement ancilla whose position becomes correlated with the battery's energy. The scheme is amenable to experimental implementation in trapped-ion systems, and it contributes to the ongoing efforts to understand the role of quantum coherence and measurement in thermodynamics.

</details>


### [144] [Subspace Controllability in Variational Quantum Circuits: Maximising Expressivity and Increasing Search Efficiency with Dynamical Lie Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24475)
*Andrew Rowan Barlow,Hans-Martin Rieser,Markus Lange*

Main category: quant-ph
