Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出物理信息潜神经算子框架，用于三维压气机叶栅流场预测，结合Transolver潜编码器与PINNs解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维压气机叶栅流场CFD计算成本高，需要高效且具泛化能力的代理模型，以支持涡轮机械气动预测。

Method: 采用Transolver潜编码器从点云CFD数据提取流况的紧凑全局潜表示，配合基于坐标的PINNs解码器重建连续流场，并在训练中引入物理信息残差约束。

Result: 框架能准确重建复杂三维叶栅流的压力与速度分布，在未见工况下仍保持良好预测能力。

Conclusion: 该方法在高效CFD代理建模与涡轮机械气动预测方面具有应用潜力。

Abstract: A physics-informed latent neural operator framework is proposed for three-dimensional compressor cascade flow prediction. The proposed method combines a Transolver-based latent encoder, which extracts compact global latent representations of flow conditions from point-cloud CFD data, with a coordinate-based PINNs decoder to reconstruct continuous flow fields while incorporating physics-informed residual constraints during training. The results show that the proposed framework can accurately reconstruct pressure and velocity distributions of complex three-dimensional cascade flows and maintains good prediction capability under previously unseen operating conditions. The present method demonstrates promising potential for efficient CFD surrogate modelling and aerodynamic prediction in turbomachinery applications.

</details>


### [181] [The Positive Defect Problem: Target and Admissibility Criteria for a Programmatic Search for Unforced Navier-Stokes Blowup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23868)
*Jarret Petrillo,James Glimm*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文为无外力Navier-Stokes千禧问题（命题(A)(B)）设定一个可搜索目标：正缺陷问题，即周期立方体上光滑初值的Leray-Hopf解在固定粘性下于有限时间窗内损失超出粘性耗散的能量；正缺陷蕴含爆破，从而否定命题(B)。文中证明该目标等价于Littlewood-Paley壳层能流的下界，并给出候选解的必要条件，用128³与256³伪谱搜索验证了细壳能流下界的约束性。所有未标注的蕴含均在Lean 4/Mathlib中形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有外力问题的程序化搜索已产出了奇点以及Clay问题的命题(C)(D)，但无外力情形（命题(A)(B)）仍开放，且缺乏明确的搜索目标。本文旨在为无外力问题固定一个可操作的目标——正缺陷问题，以便程序化搜索有明确靶向。

Method: 定义正缺陷问题：周期立方体上光滑初值的Leray-Hopf解在固定粘性、有限时间窗内损失超出粘性耗散的能量。将其等价地刻画为Littlewood-Paley壳层能流（Onsager湍流理论中粗粒化能流的Fourier侧形式）在窗口上的平均下界。给出候选解的必要条件与不可作为认证手段的说明。用128³与256³伪谱数值搜索检验归约中哪一条件起约束作用。相关蕴含在Lean 4/Mathlib中形式化，库中不含Navier-Stokes对象，方程仅以假设形式进入。

Result: 正缺陷等价于壳层能流下界；候选解须满足：速度上为Type II奇异时间、能量集中于零长度集合、压力不在L²、速度不在Onsager临界类L³_t B^{1/3}_{3,c_0}、且存在对坍缩到固定稳态Euler剖面的阻碍。不可作认证：有限计算无法见证Galerkin一致上界，选择与外力会把问题退回正缺陷。伪谱搜索显示：细壳能流下界在约三分之一翻转时间内维持在ceiling的1%–6%以内，但在Kolmogorov波数处尺度上失效，故候选解须在级联深度而非时间上与一般湍流不同。

Conclusion: 正缺陷问题为无外力Navier-Stokes问题提供了一个明确的搜索目标：它蕴含爆破（否定命题(B)），并等价于Littlewood-Paley壳层能流下界。必要条件刻画了候选解的奇异结构与不可认证手段，数值结果指出关键约束在于级联深度而非时序。相关工作在Lean 4/Mathlib中形式化，方程仅通过假设引入。

Abstract: On 7 and 8 September 2026 programmatic search produced singularities: a forced Navier-Stokes singularity at every fixed viscosity, statements (C) and (D) of the Clay problem, and two Euler singularities. The unforced problem, statements (A) and (B), stands open, and a search for it needs a target. This paper fixes one: the positive defect problem, that a Leray-Hopf solution from smooth data on the periodic cube loses energy on a finite window, at fixed viscosity, beyond what viscosity removes. A positive defect implies blowup and so a negative answer to statement (B); the converse is not known. The paper proves the target equivalent to a floor on the energy flux through the Littlewood-Paley shells, the Fourier-side form of the coarse-grained flux of the Onsager theory of turbulence, averaged over the window; states necessary conditions on a candidate: a singular time of Type II in velocity, energy concentrating on a set of zero length, a pressure outside L^2, a velocity outside the Onsager-critical class L^3_t B^{1/3}_{3,c_0}, an obstruction to collapse onto a fixed steady Euler profile; and states what cannot certify one: no finite computation witnesses a Galerkin-uniform ceiling, and selection and forcing return the question to a positive defect. A pseudo-spectral search at 128^3 and 256^3 shows which condition of the reduction binds: the fine-shell flux floor holds to within 1 to 6 percent of the ceiling for a third of a turnover time, and fails in scale at the Kolmogorov wavenumber, so a candidate must differ from generic turbulence in the depth of its cascade, not in its timing. Every implication not marked otherwise is a theorem in Lean 4 over Mathlib; the library contains no Navier-Stokes object, and the equation enters only through hypotheses.

</details>


### [182] [Mechanism-Separated Closed-Form Transition Modeling via Field Inversion and Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24162)
*Seunghyun Joo,Younghyo Kim,Kwanjung Yee*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出基于场反演与符号回归的"机制分离"转捩模型，对自然、横流、分离诱导转捩分别建立显式闭式修正项，修正SA方程生成项，无需额外输运方程或运行时神经网络推理，并在独立求解器与三维复杂构型上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新一代飞行器、旋翼与风力机对气动效率要求提高，减阻成为核心设计目标；在转捩敏感构型中，层流区范围显著影响黏性阻力与性能，因此需准确预测层流-湍流转捩。然而基于输运方程的转捩模型增加计算成本与实现复杂度，神经网络闭包难以解释且不易集成到独立求解器，而单一紧凑修正难以在异构转捩数据上保留各机制的特定行为。

Method: 采用场反演（field inversion）结合符号回归（symbolic regression）构建机制分离的转捩模型：将自然转捩、横流转捩与分离诱导转捩分别设置独立修正分支，得到对Spalart-Allmaras模型生成项的显式闭式修正，不引入额外输运方程、也不需要运行时神经网络推理；随后将闭式模型植入独立流动求解器以评估实现可移植性。

Result: 在典型算例与复杂三维构型（含自然层流运输机机翼与悬停旋翼）上评估，模型能够捕捉主要转捩前沿趋势及相应的气动性能变化；在悬停旋翼算例中，其墙钟时间约为同类输运方程转捩模型的55%。

Conclusion: 该机制分离的闭式转捩修正模型在保持可解释性与求解器可移植性的同时，兼顾多机制转捩预测精度与计算效率，为工程转捩预测提供了一种低成本、易集成的替代方案。

Abstract: Next-generation aircraft, rotorcraft, and wind turbines demand improved aerodynamic efficiency, making drag reduction a central design objective; in transition-sensitive configurations, the extent of laminar flow strongly affects viscous drag and performance. Accurate prediction of laminar-turbulent transition is therefore essential. Transport-equation-based transition models, however, increase computational cost and implementation complexity. Neural-network-based closures can be difficult to interpret and integrate into independent flow solvers, while a single compact correction trained on heterogeneous transition data may fail to preserve mechanism-specific behavior. This study develops a mechanism-separated transition model using field inversion and symbolic regression. The framework treats natural, crossflow, and separation-induced transition with separate correction branches and yields explicit, closed-form corrections to the Spalart-Allmaras production term, without additional transport equations or runtime neural-network inference. The closed-form model is implemented in an independent flow solver to assess implementation portability and is evaluated on canonical cases and complex three-dimensional configurations, including a natural-laminar-flow transport wing and a hovering rotor. Across the tested cases, the model captures the principal transition-front trends and associated aerodynamic-performance changes. For the hovering-rotor case, it requires about 55% of the wall-clock time of the comparable transport-equation transition model.

</details>


### [183] [Multiscale Kinetic Methods for Nonequilibrium Flow and Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24183)
*Zhaoli Guo,Kun Xu*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该综述从数值方法、计算策略、渐近性质和框架级表述四个交互层面，系统梳理近二十年多尺度动理学方法在稀薄、过渡与连续流域间连接的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡流动与输运本质上是多尺度的：连续描述在部分区域失效，而完全解析的动理学描述因数值分辨率受最小碰撞尺度限制而代价过高，因此需要多尺度动理学方法在不同流域间架桥。已有综述多聚焦于通用数值方法、渐近保持方法或特定方法族，缺少统一视角。

Method: 采用四层交互视角进行文献综述：数值方法、计算策略、渐近性质、框架级表述，并提炼贯穿各层的共同原则。

Result: 归纳出连接各方法族的共性原则：输运—相互作用耦合、渐近一致性、尺度自适应表示、尺度依赖的物理描述。

Conclusion: 多尺度动理学计算已演变为面向跨尺度非平衡系统的更广泛的输运方法论。

Abstract: Nonequilibrium flow and transport problems are inherently multiscale. Kinetic theory provides a fundamental physical basis for describing such phenomena, since it connects microscopic transport and interaction processes with emergent macroscopic behavior across regimes. In many situations, however, continuum descriptions lose validity in parts of the domain, whereas fully resolved kinetic descriptions become prohibitively expensive when numerical resolution remains tied to the smallest collision scales. Over the past two decades, a broad class of multiscale kinetic methods has therefore been developed to bridge rarefied, transitional, and continuum regimes in gas dynamics and other carrier-based transport systems. Existing reviews have clarified important parts of this field, including general numerical methods for kinetic equations, asymptotic-preserving methodology, and specific method families. This review adopts a different perspective by examining the subject through four interacting layers: numerical methods, computational strategies, asymptotic properties, and framework-level formulations. It surveys the main developments along these lines and emphasizes the common principles that connect them, including transport-interaction coupling, asymptotic consistency, scale-adaptive representation, and scale-dependent physical description. From this perspective, multiscale kinetic computation has evolved into a broader transport methodology for nonequilibrium systems across scales.

</details>


### [184] [Quantum many-body framework for passive-scalar turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24284)
*Zhaoyuan Meng,Long Wang,Guowei He*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文提出非厄米玻色子框架，用连续Wegner流统一湍流的等时异常统计与多时关联，并在Taylor-Kraichnan模型中给出四阶双时零模标度函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间结构如何在多时关联中体现是湍流的基本问题；现有等时异常统计与时间传播缺乏统一的算符描述。

Method: 构建非厄米玻色子框架，在扩展波频空间中用连续Wegner流重组随机模耦合；应用于Taylor-Kraichnan模型，通过传播子作用于逐级缩减的活跃场集合来构造多时贡献，并采用各向同性径向闭合。

Result: 重现已有等时层级结构；将均匀输运与内禀相对动力学分离；给出显式四阶双时零模标度函数，刻画相对色散对初始间距敏感性的逐步减弱。

Conclusion: 该框架通过观测时间序列决定等时异常结构的传播，从而把空间间歇性与时间演化联系起来。

Abstract: How spatial structures manifest in multi-time correlations is a fundamental question in turbulence. We develop a non-Hermitian bosonic framework that unifies equal-time anomalous statistics and their temporal propagation within a common operator representation. A continuous Wegner flow reorganizes stochastic mode couplings in an extended wave-frequency space. Applied to the Taylor-Kraichnan model, the framework recovers the established equal-time hierarchy and constructs multi-time contributions through propagators acting on successively smaller sets of active fields. This construction separates uniform transport from intrinsic relative dynamics and shows how a higher-order equal-time state evolves under a generator acting only on the fields that remain dynamically active. Within an isotropic radial closure, we derive an explicit fourth-order two-time zero mode scaling function that describe how relative dispersion progressively weakens sensitivity to the initial separation. The framework thus connects spatial intermittency to temporal evolution by identifying how the sequence of observation times determines the propagation of equal-time anomalous structures.

</details>


### [185] [Impact of a resonator on vortex induced vibrations of a wind turbine airfoil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24331)
*Sergio Horcas,David Roca,Enrique Ortega,Juan Cante*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过流固耦合数值模拟研究局部共振超材料（LRM）用于风力机叶片涡激振动（VIV）抑制的可行性，发现共振器仅在特定频带内有效，且在真实质量比下减振效果有限，实现50%振幅衰减需质量比大于0.25。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡激振动源于钝体脱落与结构动力学的耦合，对多个工业领域有不利影响，需要探索新型VIV抑制策略，本文尝试将局部共振超材料应用于风力机叶片的VIV抑制。

Method: 将问题简化为弹性支撑的经典翼型横向振荡；采用具备任意拉格朗日-欧拉（ALE）能力和内置刚体运动求解器的开源有限体积代码建立并验证流固耦合模型；通过定制开发将单个共振器的动力学耦合进主系统；以共振器频率和相对翼型质量为参数化变量评估不同设计性能。

Result: 识别出共振器能有效影响振动幅值的频带；但在真实相对共振器质量下振幅衰减很低，实现50%振幅衰减需要质量比大于0.25；机理分析表明减振效果来源于共振器引入的气动弹性频率分岔。

Conclusion: 局部共振超材料对VIV抑制的效果受质量比限制，在现实质量条件下减振能力有限；但其作用机制（气动弹性频率分岔）得到阐明，为VIV抑制替代技术的未来应用提供了基础。

Abstract: The aeroelastic instability known as vortex induced vibrations (VIV) has its origin in the coupling of bluff body shedding and the structure dynamics, and can have adverse effects on several industrial fields. In this work the use of locally resonant metamaterials (LRM) is explored as a wind turbine blade VIV mitigation strategy. The problem is cast into a classical airfoil in transverse oscillation, elastically mounted. A fluid structure interaction model is then generated and validated for the main system. This is achieved using an open source finite volume code with arbitrary Lagrangian Eulerian capabilities and a built-in rigid body motion solver. Subsequently, the capabilities of the code are extended by coupling the dynamics of a single resonator into the main system through a tailored development. The performance of the resonator was evaluated for different designs, which were parameterized mainly by the resonator frequency and its relative mass with respect to the airfoil. Analysis of the results identified a frequency band where the resonator effectively influenced vibration amplitudes. However, the amplitude attenuation was very low when considering realistic relative resonator masses. In particular, achieving a 50\% amplitude reduction required a mass ratio larger than 0.25. Studying the mechanisms uncovered the cause of these beneficial effects, which relied on the aeroelastic frequency bifurcation introduced by the resonator. These fundamental findings open the door to future applications of alternative technologies for VIV suppression.

</details>


### [186] [Bubble detachment from circular cavities and flat surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24342)
*Ianto Cannon,Stefan Endres,Lutz Mädler,Marc Avila*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 通过求解Young-Laplace方程，确定附着于平面上的气泡（及悬滴）最大稳定体积与接触半径、接触角、毛细长度的关系，并识别三种脱离模式；预测值与电解、沸腾、碳酸气泡及悬滴实验吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气泡（及悬滴）附着于表面时的最大稳定体积与其脱离条件在电解、沸腾、碳酸化等过程中具有重要实际意义，但已有分析估计较为零散，需要统一且更精确的定量框架。

Method: 以Young-Laplace方程为控制方程，采用打靶法（shooting method）数值求解气泡的平衡形状，并据此判定三种脱离模式：颈部收缩（necking at the cavity）、侧向失稳（sideways instability）、铺展后在周围表面颈部收缩（spreading followed by necking）。

Result: 计算得到最大稳定体积随接触半径、接触角和毛细长度的变化规律；预测的脱离体积与电解气泡、沸腾气泡、碳酸气泡以及悬滴的实验测量结果吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该工作整合并拓展了此前的解析估计，给出统一的气泡/悬滴脱离体积预测模型；由对称性，结果亦直接适用于悬滴。

Abstract: We determine the maximum stable volume of bubbles attached to flat surfaces as a function of contact radius, contact angle, and capillary length. By solving the Young-Laplace equation using a shooting method, we calculate equilibrium bubble shapes and determine when bubbles detach through three distinct modes: necking at the cavity, sideways instability, and spreading followed by necking on the surrounding surface. Our predicted detachment volumes agree well with experimental measurements of electrolytic, boiling, and carbonated bubbles, as well as pendant drops. These results bring together and extend previous analytical estimates and, by symmetry, also apply directly to pendant drops.

</details>


### [187] [A closed-form solution for streaming and Lagrangian transport in a deforming circular cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24368)
*Zijian Liu,Julian Olszewski,Yang Lin,Yuan Gao,Mengren Wu,Jie Xu*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文在二维圆形腔体内，针对变形壁面驱动的黏性主导流动（Wo²→0），给出任意角向模态 m 的拉格朗日平均流闭式解：无滑移壁面下 ψ_L ∝ m(5m+4)/(128(m+2)(2m+1))·r^{2m}(r²-1)²sin2mθ，并讨论细胞中心位置、峰值标度、模态排序及有限 Wo 的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变形腔壁产生的流动在微混合、微泵浦和颗粒操控中有重要应用，但此前缺乏对任意角向模态、黏性主导极限下平均流的解析刻画；作者希望给出闭式解以揭示壁面运动学对流动结构的普适标度规律。

Method: 在二维圆形腔体内求解黏性主导极限（Wo²→0，斯托克斯层覆盖整个腔体）。对雷诺应力强迫做双调和反演，并引入运动壁面施加的二阶滑移修正，得到示踪粒子所遵循的拉格朗日平均流；同时对无滑移壁面和自由滑移（无剪切）界面分别给出流函数。有限 Wo 时一阶保持贝塞尔函数闭式、二阶化为积分。另用独立有限元求解器（不预先知道闭式解）进行验证。

Result: 得到无滑移壁面闭式解 ψ_L = -[m(5m+4)a_m²/(128(m+2)(2m+1))] r^{2m}(r²-1)² sin2mθ，以及自由滑移界面对应的第二个解；比例因子 (5m+4)/(m+2) 在给定速度族中普适。m=2 时物理欧拉平均比辅助解 ψ_2 大一个量级且符号相反。无滑移涡胞中心位于 r²=m/(m+2)；大 m 时峰值流函数按 m⁻²、峰值速度按 m⁻¹ 衰减。模态排序由壁面运动学决定：外部驱动壁面在 m=1 最大，刚性平移则驱动为零；不可伸长壳在 m=3 峰值。有限元求解器以二阶收敛复现 ψ_2，并恢复 Rayleigh 系数 -3m/8。

Conclusion: 该构造给出了变形腔壁驱动平均流的完整解析框架，揭示涡胞位置与模态标度的普适规律，并可推广到模态叠加与有限 Wo 情形，经独立数值方法验证，对微流控中的混合与输运设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: Streaming from a deforming cavity wall serves micromixing, pumping and particle handling. We solve it in closed form in a two-dimensional circular cavity, for any azimuthal wall mode $m$, in the viscous-dominated limit $\mathrm{Wo}^2 \to 0$, where the Stokes layer spans the cavity. A biharmonic inversion against the Reynolds-stress forcing, corrected by the second-order slip a moving wall imposes, gives the Lagrangian mean a tracer follows as an elementary streamfunction for a deforming no-slip wall, $ψ_L = -[m(5m+4)a_m^2/(128(m+2)(2m+1))]\,r^{2m}(r^2-1)^2\sin 2mθ$, and a second for a shear-free interface. The factor $(5m+4)/(m+2)$ relating it to the auxiliary reference-boundary solution $ψ_2$ is universal across the prescribed-velocity family; at $m=2$ the physical Eulerian mean peaks an order of magnitude above $ψ_2$ and with opposite sign. At every $m$ the no-slip cell centers lie at $r^2 = m/(m+2)$, and at large $m$ the peak streamfunction falls as $m^{-2}$ and the peak speed as $m^{-1}$. The ranking over $m$ is set by the wall kinematics: an externally driven wall is largest at $m=1$, where rigidly translating the same circle drives nothing; an inextensible shell peaks at $m=3$. The inversion extends to mode superpositions without degenerating. At finite $\mathrm{Wo}$ the first order stays closed form in Bessel functions and the second reduces to quadrature; the construction recovers Rayleigh's coefficient $-3m/8$ on a separate tangentially driven boundary problem. An independent finite-element solver, written with the closed form withheld, reproduces $ψ_2$ with second-order convergence.

</details>


### [188] [Edge Contact Lubrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24375)
*Andrew Wilkinson,Michael A. Morgan,Michael Wilkinson*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 研究两平行平面间润滑薄膜的动力学：边界处间隙可在有限时间内降至零，最大压力发散；给出压力场在最大值附近的形态，提出高精度数值方法求解润滑面压力及水动力，绘制间隙演化相图，并讨论接触后是否滑动及滑动时运动不依赖表面粗糙度微观性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 润滑薄膜在有限时间内可能出现边界点间隙归零（接触）的情形，此时压力发散，传统分析可能失效；同时接触后的运动规律（尤其是否滑动）及其对表面粗糙度微观性质的依赖性尚不清楚，需要建立压力场刻画、数值求解与相图分析框架。

Method: 从润滑膜动力学出发，分析间隙趋于零时压力在最大值附近的形式；提出一种数值方法，用于精确确定润滑表面上的压力分布，从而计算水动力；基于初始构型与外力的作用线，构建描述间隙演化的相图；在发生接触时，进一步判断接触点是否发生滑动。

Result: 指出间隙可在有限时间内于边界某点减至零，且最大压力随间隙趋零而发散；给出最大压力附近压力场的具体形式；数值方法可准确获取润滑面压力并据此确定水动力；得到以初始构型和外力作用线为参数的间隙演化相图；当发生接触时，讨论了后续运动是否包含接触点滑动。

Conclusion: 当接触后发生滑动时，其运动不依赖于表面粗糙度的微观性质。

Abstract: In this paper we discuss the dynamics of a lubricating film separating two flat surfaces. It may be possible for the gap between the surfaces to reduce to zero at one point on the boundary in a finite time. Typically, the maximum pressure diverges as the gap between the surfaces approaches zero. We discuss the form of the pressure field in the vicinity of its maximum. We also introduce a numerical method for accurate determination of the pressure across the lubrication surface, enabling us to determine the hydrodynamic forces. We determine the phase diagram describing the evolution of the gap, as a function of the initial configuration and of the line of action of the external forces. When contact does occur, we consider whether the subsequent motion involves sliding of the point of contact. We argue that, when sliding does occur, the motion does not depend upon the microscopic properties of the surface roughness.

</details>


### [189] [High-order immersed boundary method for subsonic aeroacoustics and compressible Navier-Stokes equationsHigh-order immersed boundary method for subsonic aeroacoustics and compressible Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24729)
*Abdoulaye Ouattara,David Marx,Christian Prax,Véronique Fortunée,Manuel Diaz,Peter Jordan*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的离散鬼点法（归属于浸入边界法），用于求解声学与气动声学问题中的线性化欧拉方程或可压缩Navier-Stokes方程。该方法基于高阶格式与两步鬼点重构：先用最小二乘插值在浸入边界法向模板上重构流场，再用四阶拉格朗日外推重构鬼点值以施加边界条件。通过压力脉冲撞击圆柱的声学问题及多个雷诺数下圆柱绕流进行了验证与确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算流体力学中，采用笛卡尔网格（如有限差分法）时，浸入边界法是处理复杂几何的经典技术。现有不同浸入边界方法各有局限，作者希望针对声学与气动声学问题提出一种新的离散鬼点法，以在高阶格式下准确施加浸入边界条件。

Method: 提出新的离散鬼点法，属于浸入边界法的一种。基于高阶格式与鬼点法，鬼点处的流场值分两步确定：首先使用最小二乘插值，在位于浸入边界法向的模板点上重构流动；然后基于与边界点相邻的法向模板点进行四阶拉格朗日外推，通过重构鬼点处的场值来施加浸入边界上的边界条件。方法面向线性化欧拉方程或可压缩Navier-Stokes方程求解声学与气动声学问题。

Result: 该方法通过压力脉冲撞击圆柱的声学问题进行了验证与确认；同时对多个雷诺数下的圆柱可压缩绕流进行了验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的离散鬼点法可有效用于声学与气动声学问题，在笛卡尔网格下结合高阶格式处理复杂几何，并通过声学算例与多个雷诺数下的可压缩绕流算例验证了其有效性与准确性。

Abstract: In computational fluid dynamics, the immersed boundary method is a classical technique to account for complex geometries when Cartesian grids are used, as is the case in finite difference methods. The present paper presents a new discrete ghost point method, which is one of the different immersed boundary methods available, for solving problems in acoustics and aeroacoustics with the linearized Euler equations or the compressible Navier-Stokes equations. This method is based on high-order schemes and a ghost point method. The flow field values at the ghost points are determined in two steps. A least-squares interpolation is first used to reconstruct the flow on a stencil of points located on the normal to the immersed boundary. Then, a 4th order Lagrangian extrapolation based on the normal stencil points adjacent to the boundary point is used to impose the boundary conditions at the immersed boundary by reconstructing the field values at the ghost points. This approach is verified and validated for the acoustical problem of a pressure pulse impinging on a cylinder. The approach is also validated for compressible flow past a circular cylinder at several Reynolds numbers.

</details>


### [190] [A high-order ghost-point immersed boundary method coupled with auxiliary differential equations for time-domain impedance walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24785)
*Abdoulaye Ouattara*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 将鬼点浸没边界法与辅助微分方程耦合，在笛卡尔网格上施加局部反应阻抗边界条件，用于线性化欧拉方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非贴体笛卡尔网格上施加局部反应阻抗边界条件（如壁面声衬）存在困难，需要一种高精度且稳定的数值方法。

Method: 采用鬼点重构（法向模板法）：以椭圆最小二乘流体节点云沿局部壁面法向构造高阶多项式，再用一维拉格朗日插值外推到鬼点；阻抗以质量-弹簧-阻尼振子建模，每个鬼点引入两个辅助状态变量随时间推进，并在每个Runge-Kutta阶段与重构耦合。

Result: 在平面浸没壁、圆柱壁反射声脉冲和圆柱散射谐波单极子算例上与精确解及半解析参考对比。曲壁收敛阶约3，平壁约4.5；与精确谐波参考交叉验证表明，与半解析参考间的持续偏差源于该参考本身而非耦合求解器。半离散线性算子的谱稳定性分析表明，法向模板重构减轻但未消除浸没壁与网格相切时出现的已知不稳定。

Conclusion: 该方法能以高阶精度在非贴体笛卡尔网格上施加局部反应阻抗边界条件，验证了精度并揭示了切向相切情形下的稳定性特性。

Abstract: A ghost-point immersed boundary method is coupled with auxiliary differential equations to impose a locally-reacting impedance condition on a wall that does not conform to a Cartesian grid, in the framework of the linearized Euler equations. The ghost-point reconstruction follows the normal-stencil approach: an elliptical least squares cloud of fluid nodes is used to build a high-order polynomial along the local wall normal, which is then extrapolated onto the ghost points by one-dimensional La grange interpolation. The impedance, modeled as a massspringdamper oscillator, is advanced in time through two auxiliary state variables per ghost point, coupled to the reconstruction at every RungeKutta stage. The method is validated against exact and semi-analytical references on a flat immersed wall, a cylindrical wall reflecting an acoustic pulse, and a cylinder scattering a harmonic monopole. Convergence orders ranging from about three on the curved wall to about four and a half on the flat wall are measured, and a cross-check against an exact harmonic reference shows that a persistent offset observed against a semi-analytical reference is a property of that ref erence rather than of the coupled solver. A spectral stability analysis of the linearized semi-discrete operator further shows that the normal-stencil reconstruction reduces, without eliminating, a known instability that occurs when the immersed wall becomes tangent to the grid.

</details>


### [191] [Resolved multiple-droplet evaporation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24866)
*Alexander W Wray*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文推导了多球冠液滴（任意接触角）蒸发通量的简洁精确渐近公式，基于Kelvin变换求解点源响应并推广至高阶多极子，给出汽相屏蔽的显式表达，揭示点源问题的普适空间结构，并据此确定液流与颗粒输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多液滴蒸发中相邻液滴间的蒸汽屏蔽效应显著影响蒸发通量的空间分布，但缺乏对任意接触角、多液滴构型下空间分辨蒸发通量的简洁精确描述，也缺少对屏蔽诱导液流与颗粒输运的定量刻画。

Method: 采用Kelvin变换（球冠区域的镜像/反演方法）求解单个球冠液滴对点源的精确响应，并推广至更高阶多极子；由点源解叠加得到多球冠液滴的空间分辨蒸发通量渐近公式，进而由通量分布反推内部液流与颗粒输运，并构建屏蔽诱导液流的显式度量。

Result: 得到一个简洁而精确的多球冠液滴蒸发通量渐近公式，适用于任意接触角；给出蒸汽屏蔽的显式表达式，发现点源问题具有普适的空间结构；由通量分布确定了相应的液体流动与颗粒输运，并导出屏蔽诱导液体输运的显式度量。

Conclusion: 空间分辨的通量解析解为多液滴蒸发提供了统一描述框架，可用于定量评估屏蔽效应及其诱导的内部液流和颗粒输运，对蒸发自组装、沉积图案控制等应用具有指导意义。

Abstract: A simple, accurate asymptotic formula is derived for the spatially resolved evaporative flux from multiple spherical-cap droplets of arbitrary contact angle. Kelvin transforms are used to determine the exact response of a spherical-cap droplet to a point source, with extensions to higher multipoles. This yields explicit formulae for vapour shielding and reveals a universal spatial structure in the point-source problem. Resolving the spatial variation enables the determination of the associated liquid flow and particle transport. The resulting flux is used to derive an explicit measure of shielding-induced liquid transport.

</details>
